- Texture match after: the comparison (checklist.js), the match bar with Match close-up / Undo (which also checks the section), the white-paper line with Retake close-up, the blurry note (no-photo cards), Replace close-up (the chooser with Remove close-up on touch) and, without touch, Remove close-up (`.ckrm`), then Read color and texture (`rdAddBtn`, `rdCostLine`, `rdDoneHtml`) and Fine as is
- Pattern: Claude's pick (`q.aiTag.pattern`) and its second choice (`q.altPattern`) as drawn tiles (`.ckpt`, `role=radio`, `swatchSVG(patVar(...),true)`; "Your choice" when another is set), then "All patterns" (the picker, `patBtn`)
- Fabric & fit: metal / buckle, cuffs, vest, season, kind, style, leather, the watch's fields, Material with Claude's suggestion (`matRow` … `q.matAI`: "Material suggested by Claude"), and the Fabric swatch zoom / position box (`.sw-box`)
- More: Name, Brand, Notes (no longer behind "More details"), Bought, on the way (`orderRow`) and How many (`qtyRow`)
- the footer line "N left to check · you can still confirm" / "All M checked · ready to confirm" (`ckLeftLine`, `.ckleft`) over Confirm and Skip / Cancel (in `#nps-bar` on the no-photo sheet); Confirm is never blocked by unchecked parts, only while Claude reads or it saves (`confBtn`)
- from 600px a list card is wider than the page's 560px column (`min(100vw - 32px, 880px)`, centered)
- the old "Step 2 · Fabric close-up" (`texSlot`) and the card's "More details" fold (wardrobe.js `moreRow`, kept unused) are gone
### `itemsheet.js`

#### Photos

- the Wardrobe editor can replace, rotate, zoom-crop and undo an item's photo without touching its tags (`photoTools`, `setPhoto`)
- the photo tools are one row right under the pictures, always shown (2026-11-15, edit-photos-mockups option B, the owner's go; the "Edit photos" fold, `togglePhotoTools` and `phEdit.open` are gone): Replace · Rotate · Crop · Close-up (`.phtools`, `.ptbtn` with `.pt-replace`/`.pt-rotate`/`.pt-crop`/`.pt-closeup`), each a drawn icon (`PT_ICON`) over a short label, 58px tall (3.625rem), as many columns as buttons; full names for screen readers ("Replace photo", "Rotate the photo a quarter turn clockwise", "Zoom and crop the photo", "Replace close-up" / "Add fabric close-up": `aria-label`, on the file input for the label-wrapped ones); no photo: "Add photo" alone (plus Close-up); not a fabric: no Close-up; `tests/editphotosb.test.js`
- the row's Close-up is `closeupBtn` (ui.js, which takes `aria` for the name and markup in `label`): on a touch screen the close-up chooser (Take / Choose / Remove / Cancel); without touch the file window itself, with "Remove close-up" as a quiet 44px button under the row (`.ptmore .ptrm`)
- every photo change has Undo in its toast, and the editor's Undo button is gone: "Photo rotated.", "Photo replaced. …", "Photo cropped." (Apply), "Crop saved." (left without Apply), "Close-up added.", "Close-up removed." (`setPic` adds Undo to a plain-text message; `picUndo` runs the editor's `undoPhoto` when that change is still the last on the item open, else puts the picture back itself); Replace's and Rotate's Undo also put back the sharp copy and the pattern's box (`photoUndoAct`)
- under the row, for fabrics, the "Color & texture" card (`#ivct`, `.ivct`, a card like Details): "Claude reads them from your close-up.", the paper line (`paperLine`) when it applies, "True color in 3 steps", then "Read color and texture from close-up" with its cost and the reading's done card (`rdEditorBtn(i)` with nothing after); the 3 steps and the paper line are on `--bg`, set off from the card
- an item's photo is kept at `PH_MAX` 1536px (JPEG .85; the owner, 2026-11-13: "upgrade the main photo size stored as well"), by Add too (`cardPics`); photos are stored one by one on the server and fetched when needed (sync.js), so the backup's text doesn't grow with them
- every item photo has a small copy for grids and lists (`photoSm`, `PH_SM` 480px, JPEG .82, with `phSig` = the photo's signature `phSigOf`): `phSm(i)` gives it when current, else the photo; the wardrobe grid, the piece picker (`pickPic`) and stats (`statPic`) use it, the item sheet, the pieces list's photo and the photo generator the full photo; `makeSmall` makes it on save, in `setPic` and `undoPhoto`, and `smallPhotos` at start-up for any item without a current one; synced like any photo
- `upgradePhotos` (tagging.js, at start-up after `upgradeCloseups`, once: localStorage `photo_up` '1536'): a photo under `PH_MAX` whose sharp copy on this device (`tagImgOf`) shows the same picture (`cropSource`'s test) is made again at `PH_MAX` from it, in the old photo's colour (each channel's linear mean matched, gains 0.6–1.6: the Add card white-balanced the old one); a cropped or replaced photo doesn't match and is left; a toast says how many; `tests/photoedit.test.js`
- pictures remade at start-up are spread over days on a connected phone (2026-11-16, the audit's W1-1-02: an update remaking every photo cost about 3 server writes an item on one morning): `upgradePhotos` (2 a photo: it and its small copy), `upgradeCloseups` (1) and itemsheet.js `smallPhotos` (1) stop at `REMAKE_DAY` (10) a day (tagging.js `remakeLeft`/`remakeSpend`, `localStorage.remake_day` {d, n}); the upgrades set their once-flag only when nothing was held back, and their toast says the rest follow; a phone with no connection (`syncNeedsPull` false) has no limit
- zoom & crop cuts from the best copy (`cropSource`): the sharp copy kept on this device when the photo was read (`tagImgOf`, up to 2048px), turned to match the photo, when it is the same picture (same shape, mean grey difference at 24x24 up to `CROP_MATCH` 12); else from the photo; the output is still at most `PH_MAX`
- a replaced or already cropped photo no longer matches the sharp copy, so it is cropped from itself; Replace photo does not renew the sharp copy, since the pattern re-read (`rereadSett`) reads it together with the pattern box (`pbox`) measured on the original
- the editor adds, replaces and removes the fabric close-up (`replaceTexture`/`setTexture`, undo per field via `setPic`)
- the white-paper hint (under Read color and texture, see below) is "True color in 3 steps" (`paperHint` → `paperSteps(cloth)`, `.wbsteps`/`.wbstcard`; 2026-11-15, the owner's choice A from `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\paper-hint-proposals\`): Paper on an edge › Tap the paper › Take the photo, each with a 40 px drawing (the item's cloth with white paper along one edge, drawn in CSS: `.wbpic`, `--cloth` from `paperCloth(i)`; step 2 with the yellow ring and a tap, step 3 a camera)
- the whole 3-step card is one button (about 108 px tall) that opens the short tip (`paperTip` → kit.js `PAPER_CARD`, whose last line "Tips for a good close-up" opens the full guide); the old sentence and its "Tips for a good close-up" link are gone; `tests/paperstatus.test.js`, `tests/closeupsheet.test.js`
- on a touch screen the editor's close-up buttons (the row's Close-up, and Retake close-up in the paper line: `paperRetake`) open the close-up chooser (ui.js `closeupBtn`), and Remove close-up is in that sheet, not a button of its own; without touch they stay the direct file window and Remove close-up stays a button
- in the Color & texture card, for suits, blazers, trousers, shirts and ties: "Read color and texture from close-up" (colpick.js `rdEditorBtn`, `#iv-read`, `.ivrd .ivrdbtn`): the page's full-width button (height, radius, background, font as Duplicate), no icon, the cost line under it; with no close-up it opens the close-up chooser first, keeps the close-up (`replaceTexture`) and reads it
- after a close-up is added or replaced in the editor, a line at the top of the Color & texture card (`paperLine`, `#ivwb`, `.wbst`; shown while `phEdit.paper` is the close-up's `texSig`, so Undo or another item hides it): "White paper found · Colors set from it.", "No white paper · Colors may be off." or "No white paper, and the light looks warm · Colors may come out yellower than the cloth.", the last two with Retake close-up; `tests/paperstatus.test.js`
- "True color in 3 steps" is right above "Read color and texture from close-up", so it is seen before the tap (2026-11-15, the owner: "The user needs to see it before they tap the read color and texture button"; option B's mockup had Read first, the build puts the 3 steps first); on a 390×844 phone it is on the first screen above the bottom bar; `tests/editorrework.test.js`, `tests/editphotosb.test.js`
- leaving zoom & crop without Apply keeps a moved or zoomed frame (2026-11-15, the owner: "if I zoom and crop, but instead of hitting Apply I hit close, I want it to save"): Done, the backdrop, swiping the item sheet down by its header (allowed while cropping; the content still drags the picture), the phone's Back and Esc (both leave the crop with the sheet open), and ‹ › (`cropKeep` → `applyCrop(id,true)`, toast "Crop saved." with Undo, `cropUndo`); a frame left as it opened (`cropDirty`: zoom 1, centred) changes nothing; Cancel is now "Discard" (`.cropdiscard`, `cropDiscard`) and drops it; `tests/editorrework.test.js`, `tests/backgesture.test.js`
- `applyCrop` reads the frame and clears `phEdit.crop` before its first await, so the sheet may close or redraw right after; `setPic` takes a toast's text or `toast()`'s arguments, and pushes the editor's Undo only for the item the editor is on (or none)
- a picture the browser drew pure black is never saved (2026-11-15, the owner's desktop browser: its graphics failed, a zoom & crop came out black and the Light Grey suit's swatch was saved black): `deadCanvas`/`deadPic` (app.js: every channel at most `DEAD_MAX` 3 at 16x16; his Black suit's darkest swatch reads up to 26); `setPic` refuses a new photo or close-up with `DEAD_MSG` ("Your browser stopped drawing pictures… Reload the page and try again.") and returns false, so crop, rotate and replace keep the photo, its box and the sharp copy; `cropSwatch` returns null and `refreshSwatch`/`generateSwatch` keep the swatch they had; `makeSmall` keeps the old small copy; `tests/deadcanvas.test.js`
- the editor refuses a close-up under `TEX_MIN_PX` (200, add.js) on its short side with the Add card's message (2026-11-15, the re-audit: it took 40×40); `tests/ownnames.test.js`
- the phone's Back with the colour or pattern sheet up closes only that sheet (`colBackFirst`, 2026-11-15, the re-audit: Back closed Add or the item sheet underneath and left the sheet up, and "Use" changed a hidden item): a popstate listener registered here, before the item sheet's and start.js's (a capturing one on window still ran after them in Chromium), stops theirs, closes the sheet (a "New" sheet opened from the list goes ‹ back to the list first) and pushes the entry back, so `itemHist`/`appHist` still hold and the next Back closes the page; the app's own steps back (`itemBack`, `appPend`) pass; works on Add, the item sheet and Settings' "+ New"; `tests/ownnames.test.js`

#### The item sheet

- tapping an item opens it in `#item-view`, a sheet that slides up over the Wardrobe (the full width of a phone, from `--ivtop` below the top of the screen: the status bar's height on a notched phone, else 36px; a centred panel from 600px), over `#item-backdrop`; the grid never moves or reshuffles (`openItem`/`closeItem`/`renderItem`, drawn by `renderWardrobe` whenever `editing` is set)
- swiping the item sheet down closes it: its header (with the grabber) is a handle at any time, its content only while scrolled to the top (further down the swipe scrolls it back up); decided on the first move of a touch, before the browser scrolls; the sheet follows the finger and the backdrop lightens, and it closes past a quarter of its height or on a flick, else springs back (`ivDrag`, `closeItem(false, dragged)`); while cropping only by its header (and the crop is kept)
- the centred panel (from 600px) starts at `max(4vh, var(--ivtop))`, below the status bar (2026-11-15, the owner's iPhone Duo unfolded: at 4vh its grabber and the top of its header were under the status bar, where iOS takes a downward pull for Notification / Control Center, so "it doesn't let me" swipe it down; the touch code itself worked); `--sat` on `:root` holds `env(safe-area-inset-top)` so a test can stand in a status bar (`tests/sheetswipe.test.js`, phone, Duo, iPad and landscape sizes, Chromium and WebKit)
- a mouse, trackpad or Pencil drags the item sheet down by its header too (Pointer Events on `#ivhead`, `pointerType` not `touch`, a 4px dead zone before deciding), with the same follow, close and spring-back as a finger; a finger's pointer events are left to the touch listeners
- closing the item sheet on another item than the one opened (`itemFrom`) first scrolls the grid to that item's box
- the sheet also closes with Done in its bottom bar (`.ivbar`, always on screen), a tap on the backdrop (the strip above the sheet), the phone's Back button or gesture (one history entry while open: `histPush`/`histDrop`, `itemBack` marks our own `history.back()`; `histPush` waits while `appPend`) and Esc (which first leaves a crop); the photo is not a close button
- ‹ › beside Done, a sideways swipe on the sheet (it may start on a dropdown) and the arrow keys go to the previous / next item of the list on screen (`stepItem`, `wardList`); the header shows the type, the name and "n of m"
- the sheet's body (`itemBody`) is a checklist (2026-11-16, the owner's approved redesign; checklist.js): a summary line (`ivSummary`: the photo or drawing, "In rotation" / "Out of rotation" / "On the way" and "Clean in N wears" / "Due for cleaning" pills, material · season), the sample note, then sections that fold: Look & compare (fabrics, `#ivct`), Color (`colRows`, Shade), Pattern (`EP`, Pattern layout and Read pattern from photo, the outfit pictures note), Material, Details (`.ivtags`: type, metal, cuffs, vest, season, kind, style, leather, the watch's fields), Planning & care (`.ivrot`: rotation, on the way, `careCard`), Name & notes, Pictures (`.ivpics`: the photo pair with its tap-to-compare, the photo tool row), then Duplicate this item and Delete item side by side
- Look & compare is the first section and opens by default: with a close-up the comparison (checklist.js), the match bar and Match close-up, the paper line, True color in 3 steps, Read color and texture; without one "Not yet · add a close-up to compare", the 3 steps, Read color and texture (the chooser first) and "Add close-up"; its mark is ✓ when the drawing matches the cloth, ! when it is off, ○ without a close-up; Planning & care opens (with !) when cleaning is due
- the Shade row's own Match close-up is left out for fabrics (Look & compare has it)
- while cropping, the crop frame takes the summary's place at the top (`startCrop` scrolls there); the Pictures section says so
- from 600px the panel is `min(900px, 96vw)` wide with two columns (`.ivcols`): the summary and Look & compare (Pictures for non-fabrics) on the left, the rest on the right; under 600px one column in the order above (`display:contents`, `order`)
- `optLab(list, v)`, `ivDetailSum`, `ivCareSum` make the summaries
- while the sheet is up, `header`, `main` and `nav` are `inert` (`itemShown`), `body.iv-on` lifts the toast and the busy pill above the bottom bar, and `show()` of another tab drops the sheet (`dropItem`)
- `startCrop` scrolls the item sheet to the top, where the crop frame replaces the picture
- the phone's Back in the crop frame leaves the crop as Esc does (keeping a moved or zoomed frame, `cropKeep`) and keeps the sheet open (the item sheet's popstate listener puts its entry back); the next Back closes the sheet (the Back audit, 2026-11-15: it closed the sheet)
- an item with a photo and a close-up gets a tappable pair (`#ivpair`, class `cmpable`): a tap on the split swatch splits them up (`toggleCompare`, `phEdit.cmp`, class `cmp`): a copy of the drawing (`.cmpdraw`, hidden on the swatch's drawn half) slides over the photo's square and the close-up's clip opens to its whole square; a tap on either square puts them back; the captions switch to "As tagged" / "Close-up"; `tests/compare.test.js`
- out of rotation: the Rotation card has "Take out of rotation" / "Put back in rotation" (`togglePause`); taking it out releases upcoming days it was pinned to (`force`), the picker and "Suggest another" leave it out (`slotPool`/`swapSlot` in history.js; correcting a past day still offers it), and its grid box is dimmed with an "Out of rotation" tag (`.gi.paused`, `.pausetag`)
- the sheet follows a sync (fix round 3, W1-5-04, W1-6-02; `itemSynced`, called by sync.js `applyMerged`): the open piece changed on another phone is drawn again at once (`renderItem`), or, while a field in the sheet is typed in, once it is left; deleted on another phone it says so in a toast and a line at the top (`.ivgone`) and stays open until closed (redrawn it would vanish under the finger); a change made in it then says it wasn't kept (`itemGoneTell`, from `sete`, `togglePause`, wardrobe.js `setItemField`)
- "Take out of rotation" / "Put back in rotation" carry what the button said (`togglePause(id, want)`): when the piece is already that way it only redraws and says so, never the opposite
- a type change drops what the new type has no field for (`typeTidy`, `TYPE_FIELDS`; 2026-11-15, the new-user audit: a raincoat made cufflinks was titled "· warm weather"): season, metal, leather, cuffs, vest, kind, style, the watch's options, a material not in its list (a typed Other stays where the type has a list), a pattern reading on a type without patterns, and a colour it can't show (`colTidy`); a new metal type gets silver
- "Read pattern from photo" is offered only with a photo or close-up; without one the sheet says "Add a photo above to read its pattern from it." (`.nopatpic`), and the Outfit pictures note (`asmGapNote`) says to add a photo first
- a belt's metal is "Buckle" here as on the Add card; the metal 'other' reads "Gunmetal" in the dropdowns (`METAL_OPTS`, core.js)
- adding a close-up toasts "Close-up added." (the realistic photos it promised are no longer made)

#### The Wardrobe grid

- swiping the grid or ←/→ steps through the type filters in chip order, skipping empty types (`stepType`/`wardTypes`)
- Wardrobe boxes are one tap target (photo, type eyebrow `.gtype`, name) with no buttons; Delete (`.danger`) is in the item sheet; Add and the editor use one set of labels (Type, Colour, Second colour, Third colour) and dropdown labels start with a capital (`cap1`, app.js)
- `renderWardrobe` draws no header row (2026-11-15, the owner: the title and count weren't needed): a visually hidden `h2` "Wardrobe" and item count (`.vh`; `#wcount`, `role=status`) for screen readers, then `wardBar()` at the top of the page, the chips and the grid through `wardGrid(wardFresh())` (wardrobe.js)
- an empty Wardrobe shows only its empty card: no search field, sort or type chips

### `history.js`

#### The score card

- the score card is a `#sbox` fold box like `#ibox`/`#mbox` (`scoreCard`, `toggleScore`, `scoreOpen`; its checks slide in one after another and each `.sbar` fills to the points scored)
- the score card's second line says what the score means (`scoreCard`, `adviceFor`, memoised per outfit): 'N things to put right' with a hard check (the badge turns red), 'Could be N with what you own' when a swap scores higher, 'The best your wardrobe allows' below 100 with none, else 'Outfit score'
- unfolded, swaps that would score higher sit above the checks as 44px rows (`.sfix`, `.sfixbtn`: swatch, piece, +points; a tap wears it through `applyFix` → `pickItem`); a hard check is marked '!' in red, a check nothing owned improves says so (`.lim`), and a capped score explains the cap (`.scap`); none of this on past or worn days
- no piece card under the score
- the score box and the pieces list unfold and fold like the month (`boxUnfold`/`boxFold`, `.bfast`, `.bshut`): laid out once, the card's edge moving over it and a slide of what is under it; their own entry cascades (the checks and bars, the pieces) still play; folding lifts the rows out last first (`.blift`, `.bli` with `--bi`, `--bn`; `BOX_LIFT` 20 ms apart, `mcLift`), then the card closes; `box._fa` holds a box's running animations so a tap mid-fold finishes it (`boxStop`); `tests/boxfold.test.js`, `tests/scoreanim.test.js` (measures the visible height)
- the month, score and pieces cards don't use a `clip-path` for their moving edge: `foldEdge(box, from, to, o)` lays a strip over the card (`.fedge` clipped to the card's shape, its `<i>` drawing the rounded bottom border with `var(--bg)` under it; `.fedge-on` makes the card `position:relative; overflow:visible; display:flow-root` meanwhile) that slides with the cards below; `drop()` removes it at the end or on a stop
- why: Safari repaints an animated clip-path on the page's thread every frame while it slides transforms on its compositor, so on an iPhone the card's edge and the cards below drifted apart and folding juddered
- the piece photos (`picOpen`/`picShut`, today.js) use the same strip: `foldEdge` draws whatever bottom border the element has (a row's straight line, none for the tile) and fills with the first non-transparent background of its ancestors; `.fedge-bfc` (flow-root) only for an element that had overflow set, so a row's margins aren't changed
- tests find the strip with `.fedge` and wait on `getAnimations({subtree:true})` with `playState==='running'`
- Reset is a card of its own under the month (`resetCard`, `#rbox`, the cards' 76px look, "↺ Reset"), shown on a day the user changed (swaps, picks, the vest); it calls `resetDay`

#### The week's locks and re-planning

- the current week stays as shown: after each render `lockUpcoming()` gives every workday from today through the coming Friday (`lockSpan(T)` days, `LOCK_LAST` 5) `rec.lock` = `lockIds(o)` (the plan's pieces, no coat), rewritten when the plan for that day differs; a different tie today, a new item or a day marked not worn then re-plans only the days after the lock
- the lock ran seven days (`LOCK_DAYS`) until 2026-11-15; the owner: "only lock today through Friday ... a current week lock": on a Saturday the coming Friday is next week's (the next Sunday to Friday locked), on a Friday only today; only workdays get locks, so a Sun–Thu week (the owner's) and a Mon–Fri week both get the rest of their work week; days after Friday are planned freely and afresh each time, as days past seven were
- locks past the window are let go by `lockPast(end)` at the end of `lockUpcoming` (after the window is locked, so its days keep what was shown; day numbers `di`, not dates): only `rec.lock` goes, saved lock-only (`lockOnly`/`base`, as `clearLocks`); the owner's picks (force, skip, the vest, `kind`, `off`), a kept trip (`rec.trip`) and a day marked worn stay; this also undoes a phone still on the seven-day version; `tests/weeklockdays.test.js` (each weekday as today, both weeks, a day off, the release, Santiago's midnight clock change)
- `fixedAhead` and `unlockClash` look three workdays ahead whatever the window: past Friday there is no lock, so only a pick, a moved outfit or a worn day is fixed there (Pack's note: "The app keeps today through Friday as shown by itself")
- a change made by hand on a day drops its lock (`swapSlot`, `pickItem`, `setOccasion`, `resetDay`, `moveRecs`): the day is planned again around the change and locked as it then shows; the vest toggle and worn / not worn keep it
- `clearLocks()` releases the locks after today: from `setDarkShirts`, `toggleDay` (workdays, blazer days) and `setOccasion` (the days around an occasion are planned around it); `tests/weeklock.test.js`
- a new `PLAN_RULES` calls `replanFuture(true)` (the planner audit, 2026-11-14): only what the app chose goes, the week's locks (`rec.lock`) and pieces it recorded by itself (`rec.auto`); the owner's own picks (force, skip, the vest, `kind`, `off`) stay; the toast says "Your own picks were kept"; `tests/replan.test.js`
- Settings' Re-plan upcoming days (`replanFuture()`) clears the locks and the picks too, without counting them as days reset
- the planning rules a phone last planned by are its own (fix round 3, W1-1-01 = W1-4-01 = W1-2-06): localStorage and meta `plan_rules` (`PLAN_RULES_KEY`, never synced; `planRulesNote` writes both; localStorage is read first, and the database only when there is something to re-plan (a lock or a recorded piece after today), so start-up waits for nothing extra: an extra database read moved start-up's `refreshToday` into tests' animations on WebKit; with nothing to re-plan the number is just noted); `planRulesCheck` reads `settings.planRules` only when the phone has no number of its own yet (from before) and never writes it: in the synced settings a joining phone took the backup's old number and re-planned on its second start, a phone on an older version sent its lower number back with every settings save, and a roll-back brought one back; tests set `planRulesNote(n)` (or both stores); `tests/w3plan.test.js`, `tests/replan.test.js`
- a swap or pick whose new piece is held by a lock on the next workdays (its tie on any of the next three, its shirt or jacket on the next) lets go of every lock after that day (`unlockClash`, 2026-11-15): two ties, today's swapped, and the next two locked days kept that tie; no clash, nothing changes
- past workdays recorded because the app wasn't opened on them (after the first day used for real) are said once in a toast with "I didn't wear them" (`missedNote`/`missedTell`/`missedNotWorn`, 2026-11-15, the audit's week away); `tests/newuserplan.test.js`
- they also stay on Today as a card under the month on the home day (`missedCard`/`missedOpen`, `#missbox`, fix round 2: the toast was the only way): the `rec.auto` days of the last `MISSED_KEEP` (30) days after the first real use, still worn and not corrected; "I didn't wear them" marks them, "Keep as worn" hides them on this phone (localStorage `missed_ok`, the last day covered); `missedTell` puts the card in at once without drawing Today again (`missedPut`: a redraw cut off unfolding boxes, `tests/pagescroll.test.js` on WebKit); `tests/nu2plan.test.js`
- a pick, "Suggest another" or Reset on a coming day writes no pieces (`notAhead`: no `ids`, `suitDay`, `vestOn`; fix round 3, W2-17-01): they were the outfit from before the pick, and once the day passed without the app being opened `recordDays` kept them and the day stood as worn with the planner's first tie; today's record is still rewritten from the screen by `recordDays`
- a record an earlier version wrote that way is told by `idsAhead(r)` (planner.js: `ids` with `force`/`skip`, not `edited`, `auto` or `worn:true`, `updated` before the day began; no `updated`, kept): `planSim` and `fixedAhead` don't freeze the day from those pieces, and `recordDays` records it again from its picks with `auto` (so the missed-days card lists it, W2-17-18)
- `recordDays` works every record out first and puts the missed days' (after `firstReal`) in `dayRecs` synchronously, then saves them all after an `await null` (fix round 3, W2-14-12), and `renderToday` calls it before drawing (not after): the missed days count in that drawing's "last worn" pill and cleaning frame, which changed a moment after opening; the planner's first days before any real use are put in only as they are saved, after the drawing, as before; `cache.careW` is dropped with them
- correcting a past day calls `unlockClash(k, true)` (fix round 3, W2-17-06): yesterday corrected to the suit locked today lets today's lock go (the jacket on the next workday, the tie within three), so today isn't "the same suit as the workday before"
- a piece sent to the cleaner while a lock after today holds it releases the week's locks (`careUnlock(id)` from care.js `careSend`), and "It's back" (`careBack`, add.js `checkArrivals`) releases them when any was made since it left (`careUnlock(null, since)`, by `lockAt`) (fix round 3, W1-8-05: two suits away, the one locked for Tuesday was held back Sunday and Monday, and the other suit was worn both days)
- the missed-days toast is said only when the card isn't on screen (`missedTell` after `missedPut`; fix round 3, W2-14-11: both said the same at once)
- the missed-days card shows on at most `MISSED_DAYS` (3) different days for the same missed days (`missedSeen`, localStorage `missed_seen` {k: the last missed day, d: dates shown}; a new missed day shows it again; fix round 3, W1-8-04: it was on Today on 216 of 226 mornings for someone who skips a day now and then)
- with two or more days the card has "Choose days" (`missedPickToggle`, `missedPick`): one button per day (`.missone`, `missedOne(k)`) marks just that day not worn (fix round 3, W2-17-18)
- on the home day when today is a public holiday or an own day off (on a usual workday), `dayActions` says "Today is National Day, a day off. This is Sunday’s outfit." (`#offtoday`; fix round 3, W2-16-09)
- Start the plan over's question says today's outfit may change although it shows ✓ Wore, and to tap Cancel when already wearing it (fix round 3, W2-17-19; the owner's "today too" kept)
- a past day whose record has no pieces (`ids`) is recorded by `recordDays` with what the record already has: a day the app was not opened on kept only its week lock, so it never became history (no wear counts in `wornDays`, an outfit that could still change); `tests/weeklock.test.js`

#### Worn, picks and past days

- worn / not worn is one pill at the right end of the date bar's weather line on today and past workdays with an outfit (`wornPill`/`toggleWorn` in today.js, `setWorn`; the owner's design 2026-11-14, replacing the ✕ / ✓ circles on the picture): a day is worn unless marked not, so it reads "✓ Wore" (accent fill) with nothing recorded; a tap records `worn:false` ("✕ Didn't", grey) and a tap again clears it (no mark, never `worn:true`, which would freeze today's outfit); the weather stays centred (`.wx.haspill`: a three-column grid whose sides hold the pill's width `--wp`, 68 px, 58 px under 360 px), "Set your location for weather" shortens to "Set location" beside the pill; no pill on future days; the date bar's height is unchanged (`tests/todaytight.test.js`)
- "Last worn" (`lastWorn` in photos.js, a pill at the picture's bottom right, `wornBar`) counts every earlier day with recorded pieces unless marked "Didn't" (it used to take a ✓); Insights and the cleaning counts still count today only once it is over or recorded worn, as before
- the swatches on the picture no longer step up on today and past days (`swatchStack(o,false)`): the circles they cleared are gone, so the cleaning banner cuts round the tie swatch on today as on coming days
- ⇄ beside a piece opens the picker sheet (`openPicker`/`pickItem`: every item for the slot, best match first by `fitOf`; "Suggest another" is the old cycling `swapSlot`)
- the picker ranks and labels each piece by the score the outfit would get with it (fix round 3, W2-17-03: "Best match" lowered the score 93 → 81): a coming day is planned with the pick in memory only (`pickScores`: the record `pickRec` builds as `pickItem` would, planned by planner.js `planSim(...).peek(k, rec)`, which plans the days before once and then the day alone per piece, putting the rest counts back), a past day being corrected has the piece put in; kept per day, slot and plan (`pickMemo`, until `cache` is replaced); labels by score gap (`fitTag`: 0 Best, up to 4 Good, up to 12 Works); the tests' fixed plan (`TEST_PLAN`) keeps the rule-of-thumb fit (`fitRule`) and its old bands
- Suggest another never lands on a real mistake while a piece not yet shown makes a clean outfit (`suggestClean`, called from `swapSlot`; W2-17-07: a black shirt under a navy suit at 69 with lavender, in the wash, not yet offered): the best-scoring clean piece outside the cycle is pinned; nothing clean left, the planner's choice stays; browsing suggestions teaches nothing (the learning was removed, owner's decision 4)
- the picker says "Choose shoes" / "All the shoes you own…" for plural words (`pluralWord`: shoes, pants, socks, cufflinks), and two pieces it would name alike get a second line (`pickSub`: brand, else material, else notes, else "2 of 2" by the order added; W2-17-13, W2-15-09)
- correcting a vest day shows no tie clip row (today.js `slots`; the vest holds the tie, W2-17-20)
- Today's short-rest warning (today.js `restWarn`, a jacket worn under `COOL` days ago) says "Add more suits/blazers" only when fewer than `COOL` of that kind are in rotation that day (not paused, not at the cleaner); on a day the owner chose it (a pick, a correction) it says "You wore this suit N days ago too."; otherwise nothing (W2-17-06: "Add more suits" with three owned)
- the score card's "… pieces were chosen for variety" line is not said on a day the owner changed (`o.overrides`: "These would score higher:"), and says "This day's" on a day other than today (W2-17-08)
- swaps and picks keep a piece taken off pinned (`keepOff`); Reset and an occasion clear `rec.off`
- swaps, picks and corrections teach the planner nothing (see "No learning from changes"); a pick or swap shows no message, a correction says "Corrected."
- a past day can be corrected to what was really worn (`togglePastEdit`/`correctPast`: the record gets `edited`, `worn:true` and the real ids)

### `today.js`

#### Layout

- tiles show a short 4:3 picture; no "Below the waist" heading
- the score, pieces and month cards share one size (76px folded, a 112px picture slot, one text column) and everything on Today is spaced by `--gap` (12px) in styles.css
- Today goes two columns (outfit left, cards right) only from 600px wide in landscape; upright (the Duo unfolded, a tablet) it stacks outfit, tiles and cards, the picture (`.fig`) the full width from 600px (under 600px it stays at most 390px, so a phone or the folded Duo fits picture and tiles on one screen); `tests/run.js` checks both
- with a wardrobe that can't dress a day yet, the "Get your first outfit" checklist (`setupToday`, setup.js) is the first thing in the stage, above the figure or the day-off note
- `goToday()` from the Today tab (the date bar's "↩ Today" / "↩ Next outfit" button is gone, the owner 2026-11-14: `todayBtn` returns ''; `tests/daypieces.test.js`); `newDayCheck()` moves on to today when the app is reopened on a later day
- a new calendar day (visibilitychange, or a minute timer while the app stays open past midnight) moves Today to `homeDay()` when it showed the old home day or any day now past (`isoKey(sel)<=isoKey(homeDay())`); a coming day the user stepped to stays (the audit's H8: opened on a Friday it still showed Friday on Sunday; `tests/nufix7.test.js`)
- start-up opens Today on `homeDay()` once the settings are read (start.js), not on the calendar day: a weekend no longer opens on an empty "Saturday isn't a workday" page
- a Settings change that moves the home day (a workday or day off taken away) moves Today with it when Settings closes (`keepHome`, from `closeSettings` before the redraw)
- a day before the wardrobe existed (`preWardrobe`: before `planStartDay()`, trimmed from the plan) says "No outfit: your wardrobe starts on …" (`#prewd`), with no score or figure (it showed a red "0 · Incomplete")
- a past day whose piece was deleted since (`o.gone`) says so under the cards (`goneNote`, `.gonenote`): the drawing can't show it
- a missing piece's row offers "Add one" (`.addone` → `openAdd()`); nothing says "Add tab" (there is none: `ADD_HINT` "Add one with the + in the Wardrobe")
- quick swipes in a row each count (`fly` in today.js: a swipe during the last one's fly-out is added to it, and the day is drawn only when no finger is down: drawing removes the element a touch started on and its moves stop coming); `stepDay(n, from, times)`
- the picture's pill says "This look last worn …" (`wornBar`): it is the jacket, shirt colour and tie together (`lastWorn`), not the suit's own last wear
- while the checklist shows (`wardShort()`: a `setupNeeds` row not ticked), a half-outfit is drawn but gets no score card, no "This look last worn" and no outfit count on the month card ("pieces still missing"); after removing the sample a suit and a shirt showed a red "50 Incomplete" (re-audit 2026-11-15)
- the date bar's weather line with the worn pill stays the centred three-column grid until the weather or "Set location" would be cut (Larger Text, page zoom: it read "Set loc…", then nothing; re-audit 2026-11-15): `wxFit()` (after each `renderToday`, and from a ResizeObserver on `#today` it installs the first time) then adds `.wxwrap`, the text takes the line alone and the pill goes under it at the right
- the "Set location" link (`.wxl`, its span `.wxlk`) is padded out to about 46 px of tap height with a matching negative margin, so nothing moves; the pill (`z-index:2`) stays above the link's padding

#### The pieces list and photos

- the pieces list is folded into `#ibox` (each piece unfolds into its photo, `toggleRow`); a below-the-waist tile under the figure unfolds that item's photo in `#bbox` below the tiles (`pickPiece`/`belowOpen`; tapping the tile again or the photo folds it)
- Today's pieces list (`slots`) has no Suit/Blazer row and no Tie / Tie clip rows for a piece the outfit lacks and the day doesn't need (`needsJacket`, `needsTie`), so no "Add one on the Add tab" for it; the rows stay while correcting a past day, to add the piece worn
- Today's pieces list, vest row and outfit chips show the drawn swatch even for an item with a close-up (`swatchSVG(i,true)`); Today's piece squares are 40px (`#today .row .dot`), as large as the rows' 40px tap height allows, so no row is taller
- a piece's photo (a row, `toggleRow`; a tile's photo, `pickPiece`/`#bbox`) opens and shuts like the boxes (`picOpen`/`picShut`, `.pfast`): laid out once, the photo's area opening while what is below it slides (`picBelow`: by position, so the side column in landscape stays), the pieces card growing with it (`picCards`); the photo drops in with the days' bounce and lifts out with their dip before the space closes; `el._pa` holds its animations (`picStop`); `tests/picfold.test.js`
- a photo is decoded before it opens (`picOpenReady`, at most 300 ms): with its height unknown the rest of it jumped in afterwards
- opening a row shuts another open photo at once (`picSnap`); one above the tapped row is made up for by the scroll, so the row stays under the finger; another tile while one is open swaps the photo in place
- folding from inside: a tap on an open photo (row or tile) folds it, a tap on the open score breakdown folds it, a tap in the open pieces list that is not on a button or photo folds the list (`foldListTap`), and folding a box whose top is off screen scrolls the card back into view (`keepInView`)
- take off for the day: − beside the jacket and the tie in the pieces list (`offBtn`, today and coming days, not worn or past days, not a "No jacket or tie" day) calls `setOff(slot, true)`, which pins the rest of the outfit (`force`, `kind`) and sets `rec.off`; the row then says "Taken off" (": the trousers stay" for a suit) with + to put it back (`offRow`, `setOff(slot, false)`); the suit's trousers row is "Trousers" with no ⇄

#### Zoom and swiping

- double-tap the picture (photo or drawing) to zoom 2.5x on the tapped spot, again to zoom out (`toggleZoom`/`applyZoom`, `zoom` state, kept across redraws of the same day); a drag pans while zoomed and doesn't change the day; `#mannequin-container` has `touch-action:manipulation` and clips the zoomed picture
- a sideways swipe folds every open box (score, list and rows, below-the-waist photo, month) as soon as the drag locks sideways, and zooms out (`foldAll`, also from `stepDay`)
- with reduced motion a day step doesn't slide (`stepDay` and the swipe's fly-out check `noMotion()`, itemsheet.js)

#### Moving an outfit in the open month

- a long press (`MOVE_HOLD` 380 ms, still within `MOVE_SLOP` 8px) on today or a later workday that isn't worn and has no occasion (`data-mv` on the cell) lifts its drawing (`.mv-ghost`, held above the finger; the other days wiggle, `#mpanel-in.mv-on`); the mouse holds the button
- while it is dragged, the day under it previews the month re-planned with the outfit there (`moveRecs` pins its pieces on the target with its `kind`, clears the day it left; `planWith` runs `buildPlan` on a copy of `dayRecs`); `showPlan` redraws only cells whose outfit changed (`mvSig`), sliding each outfit from the cell that showed it (FLIP), and nothing is saved until the drop
- dropping saves both days, re-renders and toasts what moved with Undo (`toast(text, ms, {label, fn})`); dropping off the days, or on the day it came from, changes nothing; `outfitMove` stops the day swipe and month drag while moving
- during a move the lifted day's old drawing stays in the page hidden (`.mv-held`): removing the node a touch started on stops its touchmove/touchend; the cells' children take no pointer events (`#mpanel-in.mv-on .mc *`), since a drawing sliding to its new day caught the pointer over its old one
- a day record's `kind` ('suit' / 'blazer') fixes the day's jacket kind in `planSim`, so a suit outfit moved onto a blazer day stays a suit day; `tests/moveoutfit.test.js`

#### The plan and its snapshot

- `planMonth(y,m)` / `planFor()` keep one shared `planSim` in `cache` (anything that changes the wardrobe, history, settings or weather must reset `cache={}`)
- no outfit is shown for a day before the wardrobe existed (`planTrim` in `planMonth`: days before `planStartDay()`, the day the first item was `added`, and before today, without a record with pieces, are left out of the month's plan; the planner still plans them for rest and variety; items without `added` set no start): a new user saw two weeks of planned past days with ✕/✓ and "22 outfits"; `tests/newuser.test.js` covers it with plain errors, the list instead of 404s, captions, Several items, the setup's city search and holidays
- `hasOutfit(o)` is true for a no-jacket day too
- Today's month is kept in IndexedDB (meta `PLAN_SNAP`, `planSnapSoon` after the week is locked): pieces as `{$i: id, $x: what the planner added (rest)}` (`snapOut`/`snapIn`), about 60 KB
- at start-up `planSnapUse` puts it in `cache` when `planSig()` (the day, the items' and day records' stamps, the settings' stamp, `weather`, `climate`) matches, so Today is drawn without the planner; `cache.snap` marks it, and once idle `planSnapCheck` plans the month for real, redraws Today only if an outfit differs (`outSig`) and only then records the days and locks the week (`recordDays`/`lockUpcoming` never run from the kept copy); `planSnapUsed`, `planRuns` are read by `tests/speed.test.js`
- `wxPreload()` reads the kept forecast (`wx`, under 3 h old) and climate (`wx_norm`) before Today is first drawn, and `loadWeather` leaves the plan alone when the kept forecast is what it already has: with a location set, start-up planned three times (without weather, with the climate, with the forecast)
- measured (emulated Pixel 7, CPU 4x, 107 items): warm start ready 662 → 205 ms, longest task 302 → 78 ms

### `month.js`

- the month plan is folded into `#mbox` and unfolds in place; the open month has a full-width `.foldbar` ("Fold away") right under the days
- the open month has no arrows, swipe hint or legend (the cells show worn/today/planned/rest themselves), its centred title close above the weekday row: a sideways touch swipe inside `#mpanel-in` (handed over by the Today swipe handler, `st.month`) or a mouse drag moves between months (`monthDrag`/`monthRelease`, `monthStep(n, from)` slides the new month in; the `#mswipe` wrapper moves); the day doesn't change and nothing else folds; a drag's closing click doesn't open a day
- the month box unfolds and folds without animating its height (`monthUnfold`/`monthFold`): the page is laid out once (`.mfast` turns the height, padding and per-day transitions off), the card's edge (`foldEdge`), a slide of what is under it (the Reset card) and a fade of the days move; measured at 4x CPU: unfold 30 layouts and 285 paints before, 5 and 7 after; fold 32/56 before, 4/4 after
- folding scrolls first to where the shorter page can stay (no jump when it is laid out shut) and turns the arrow back at once (`.mshut`); `monthStop` finishes a fold interrupted by another tap
- the days are put into the folded box while the app is idle (`monthFillSoon` from `monthLink`, `monthFill`, `data-fill` = month and selected day), so a tap only unfolds it; the folded `#mpanel-in` is `inert`
- the days drop in one after another with a little bounce (`mcDrop`, easing `cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)`, 12 ms apart) while `.mdrop` is on the box, only for the length of an unfold (`monthDrop`; not on a month swiped to, a re-plan or a move); it moves and fades only, so it adds no layout; `tests/monthunfold.test.js`
- folding plays it backwards (`.mlift`, `mcLift`, back-in easing `cubic-bezier(.36,0,.66,-.56)`): the last day lifts out first, `MONTH_LIFT` (8) ms apart, 280 ms each, a little dip before rising away; the edge starts once about half are gone and the panel's own fade only at the end (fading the whole panel earlier hid the days lifting out: it read as the month just disappearing)
- a past day marked "✕ Didn't" is a `skip` cell (stack faded, its number struck through, "not worn" in its label), not `worn`; a past workday with no outfit (before the wardrobe) is `past`, not `worn` (styles.css `.mc.skip`)
- a patterned shirt is named with its colours and pattern ("white & blue stripe shirt", `shirtWords`) in `piecesLine` and `shortRest`
- the month's drag preview is trimmed like the month (`planWith` → `planTrim`): the days before the wardrobe stayed empty only until a drag
- the month and Today's strip name a day without a jacket by its trousers ("Grey trousers") instead of "No jacket: grey trousers" when the dress code has no jackets (`shortTop`)
- every unfold and fold scrolls the page with `pageScroll(to, ms, delay)`: ONE `scrollTo` at the start, and `#today` slides back by what really scrolled (`translateY`, `MONTH_EASE`, same duration and delay, `fill:'backwards'`, held in `#today._ps`; a second call starts from the drawn offset); no script runs per frame
- why: the browser's smooth scroll kept its own pace (WebKit covered 1,000px in one frame), and a `scrollTo` on every frame from rAF ran out of step with the slide on an iPhone, and the calendar, pieces and score cards juddered; `tests/pagescroll.test.js` checks one scroll event per animation (the rAF version fired about 20)
- a box that unfolds (score, pieces list, a piece row, month plan, a tile's photo) scrolls with `reveal(el, top)`: just far enough to show what opened (`revealNeed`), stopping when the tapped box's top reaches the top of the screen
- `picfold`, `boxfold` and `monthunfold` also run on WebKit (`npm run test:webkit`): the layout count is Chromium's CDP, so there it is not counted

### `photos.js`

- item picture prompts name colors through `picCol` (`PIC_COL`: grey, light grey, dark grey, mid grey, dark slate blue-grey), not `colName`: `itemKey` hashes `itemDesc`, so the American names would have orphaned every generated picture of a gray piece (and asked to pay again); `watchText(w, cn)` takes the namer for the same reason
- a shirt with a read weave texture (`shirtTextured`) gets its own photo key (`/tex/<settSig>` in `comboSig`)
- "Last worn" (`lastWorn`) counts only days marked ✓; the stats still count unmarked past days as worn

#### Outfit photos

- outfit photos are kept on the device as the worker made them (`keptPhoto`): shrunk to 900px and re-encoded at JPEG .85 (`shrinkUrl(…,900)`) they were softer than the 1024 original on a 3x phone and when zoomed; photos kept before stay 900px until made again
- background download of the worker's photos for the month on screen and the next, nearest day first (`prefetchRenders`, after one `GET /render?list=1`)
- the worker's list is ONE request shared by photos.js and assemble.js (`workerList(force)`, kept `LIST_MS` 10 min, per server and code; 2026-11-15, the new-user audit: two lists every start, then a refused one on every swipe): `remoteList` (a copy, `remoteKeys`) and `asmListGet` both read it; a foreground or Refresh within 10 min asks nothing
- a refused list (429: the tester's or everyone's daily list allowance; 503: the server's lists failing) is not asked again until the next UTC day (`localStorage.list_pause` `{w, until}`, `listPaused`); meanwhile no day is asked about one by one either (`remoteHold` in `fetchRemoteRender`, `fetchItemPic`); a forced ask (`asmListGet(true)`: Settings' Pictures ahead) still tries once and a list that comes clears it; connecting clears it (account.js); offline, a list isn't asked again for `LIST_RETRY_MS` (1 min); `tests/nuserver.test.js`
- measured on the lab's iPhone (a new tester): 1 list per start (was 2), none on 6 swipes or a foreground; with the allowance used up, 1 refused list on the reopen and none after (was 12 in 30 s)
- a day the worker has no photo for is not asked about (`fetchRemoteRender`, `fetchItemPic`): the worker's list (`remoteList`, `GET ?list=1`, once, shared with `prefetchRenders`; `remoteListed` false when the worker gives none, then each day is asked as before) says which it keeps; each ask was a 404 logged in the console for every day shown
- outfit photos are no longer made (2026-11-14): the prompt builders (`outfitText`, `outfitRefs`, `outfitRefsFull`) were removed; kept photos still show on their days (`renderFor`) and still sync (`GET`/`PUT /render`)
- a no-jacket day's photo key includes the trousers; `comboSig` has `-` in the tie's place, so a tieless outfit has its own photo key
- `POST /render` is sent only for item pictures (`renderItemPic`, `{mode:'item'}`); the busy pill (app.js `busyLabelFor`) shows "Making the item picture…" for that and nothing for any other `/render` POST
- `picsDrawn()`: Settings → Outfit pictures is "Drawing only" (`settings.pictures==='drawn'`); then `renderFor` returns null and `fetchRemoteRender` and `prefetchRenders` ask the server for nothing
- item pictures (the tiles' product photos, made on a tap) are their own feature and are not affected by "Drawing only": a missing one is still downloaded (`fetchItemPic`, which may read `GET /render?list=1` once a session)
- an item-picture batch stops at the first refusal (`e.stop`: 401/403/429/501/503) and at the first picture the server made but couldn't keep (`stored:false`, worker/items.js): that one is kept on the phone marked `local` and uploaded later (`shareLocalPics`: at the next batch, and once a session when a list comes); the status counts what wasn't tried as "not made", not "failed" ("Done: 1 picture ready, 8 not made (…kept on this phone only…)"); before paying for a new one, `fetchItemPic(i, true)` asks the server for it by key even while lists are paused; `tests/nuserver.test.js`
- with "Drawing only", kept outfit photos and painted pictures stay on the device (nothing is deleted), so switching back shows them at once
- a refused list (401/403) that is an ended invitation (`endedAnswer`, sync.js) calls `inviteEnded`; any other refusal (the owner's mistyped code) holds every picture ask for that code for the session (`listRefused`, read by `listPaused`); with the invitation ended nothing is asked at all (`workerBase`, see sync.js); `tests/endedpics.test.js`
- the Generate item pictures question names a tester's daily limit from `/me` (`itemPicLimitText`: "You can make 18 more today, of your daily 40.", and which are made when fewer are left; `acct.me.used` from another UTC day counts 0, `itemPicsUsed`); with none left it says so instead of asking (`itemPicsUsedUp`); the owner sees no limit

#### The watch

- `watchText(w)` (core.js): with the owner's Details the style's stock layout is left out ("chronograph", not "chronograph (three subdials and a date window)"); used by the item picture's description (`itemDesc`)

#### Item pictures

- product pictures for shoes, belts, socks, pants, coats and watches are described by `itemDesc()`, made from the item's own photo by image edit when it has one, saved as `render:c<16 hex>` of the description; a Settings button generates the missing ones
- every item picture is on the same warm grey (the worker's `ITEM_BG`, worker/items.js; it ignores an old app's `bg:'white'`); product photos kept on a white backdrop are toned down to `PIC_BG` once in the app (`greyBackdrop`/`fixItemBackdrops`, flag `grey` on the render)
- a sock's `secondaryColor` is its toe cap colour (`sockToe()` in core.js): the tagger is told so, `drawSock` (draw.js) draws it, `desc` names it, the editor labels it "Toe cap"; the planner ignores it
- socks are drawn as a pair (the owner's ask, 2026-11-13), traced from Nike's product photo of their Everyday Plus Cushioned Crew socks: both socks, the back one 12% darker; no maker's mark; the toe cap and the cuff's edge take the toe colour (`class="sock-toe"`); `SOCK_D` is in a 0..100 box and the thumbnail calls `drawSock` without a transform
- the pair is drawn mirrored (`drawSock` wraps both socks in `matrix(-1 0 0 1 100 0)`) so the toes point right like the shoes (the owner, 2026-11-13); `SOCK_D` stays in the photo's own direction
- `esc()` escapes `'` too (some strings go into `onclick='…'`)

### `settings.js`

- Settings is a full-screen page (a centred panel from 600px): a sticky header with Done (`saveSettings`: saves the connection fields and closes), a Wardrobe stats link, then categories that unfold like Today's cards (`SET_CATS`: schedule, daysoff, weather, care, photos, tagging, backup, connection, about; `.scat`, `toggleCat`, `setCatOpen`), each with a one-line summary (`settingsSummaries`)
- `openSettings('<key>')` opens one category and scrolls to it; tests must open the category before clicking inside it
- `openSettings` draws each part in its own try (blocked storage threw and Settings never opened) and `saveSettings` closes Settings even when storing the server address fails (full storage), with a toast
- while Settings is open the page behind it can't scroll (`setLock`: `html.set-lock` sets `overflow:hidden` on html and body): on the owner's iPhone a scroll in Settings was handed to Today underneath, Settings stood still under the finger and Done was hard to reach; `tests/settingslock.test.js` (desktop browsers can't show the handover itself, only the lock)
- `toggleCat`'s scroll to show an opened category (460 ms after the tap) doesn't run when a finger or the wheel touched Settings since the tap (`m._touch`, set by `setLock`'s listeners): it fought a scroll already under way
- background redraws of Today (photos arriving, weather, the start-up plan check, the queue) go through `refreshToday()`: while Settings is open they set `todayStale` and wait for it to close, since `renderToday` closes Settings (it snapped shut when opened right after launch)
- a sync that brings changes redraws the tab on screen through `syncRedraw` (sync.js): while Settings is open it only marks it (`todayStale`, `viewStale` for the Wardrobe or stats page) and `closeSettings` redraws it
- only `saveUserSettings()` stamps the settings, and only something the user did should call it
- `saveUserSettings` announces each save to the app's other tabs (BroadcastChannel `dw-settings`, `setChan`); a tab that heard of a newer save (`setOther`) merges the stored copy in first (`setSeen`: the stamp it last read or wrote, set at start-up and in `onSyncedData`; `mergeSettings` setting by setting): a second tab (an invitation link tapped twice) wrote its stale copy whole over the other tab's setup choices (2026-11-15)
- without word from another tab the save is written at once: a read before every write delayed it, and on WebKit a reload right after Skip lost it (`tests/setup.test.js`)
- workdays, blazer days, the vest and units set `todayStale` too (`toggleDay`, `setVestMode`, `setUnits`), so Today follows when Settings closes; `setUnits` redraws the location line at once (`renderWxLoc`; it kept °F under °C)
- `toastDrop()` takes away a toast with a button; leaving Add (start.js `show`) drops its "Saved … Undo" (it lay over Today's month card)
- occasions (`setOccasion` in history.js, `addFormalDay`, `renderFormalUI`)
- days off (`setHolidayCountry`, `toggleHoliday`, `addDayOff`, `renderDaysOffUI`; the holiday list is refetched monthly, `refreshHolidays`); the Days off summary is redrawn by `renderDaysOffUI` and `renderFormalUI` while Settings is open
- the Schedule summary lists the days from the week's first one (the first day, Sunday first, whose day before isn't a workday): "Sun–Thu"
- weather: a 16-day forecast; beyond it `loadClimate()` fills `climate` in core.js with the location's usual monthly highs from Open-Meteo's archive, kept in localStorage `wx_norm`, so `dayClimate()` does not fall back to a temperate calendar
- the city search's results (`findCity`) are escaped (names from Open-Meteo went into the page as HTML)
- `refreshAll`'s list of pages includes `pack` and `'care'`, so Refresh stays on the Packing list and Laundry & cleaning
- a toast with an action (Undo) stays at least `TOAST_ACT_MS` (8 s) whatever its caller asks (new-user audit 2026-11-14: 5–6 s, and Delete item has no question)
- (audit 2026-11-15) dates are in the app's language: `appLocale()` makes every `Date#toLocale…String(undefined, …)` use `APP_LOC` (the phone's language when English, else `en-GB`): an Arabic-language iPhone showed Arabic dates and digits; pass a locale to opt out
- units without a choice follow the country (`unit`, `unitCC`, `F_CC`): the location's `cc` (kept by the city search) or the `, XX` at the end of its name, else `tzCountry()`, else the phone's language; a Kuwait phone in English (US) got °F
- `weekFirst()`: the work week's first day (Sun–Thu → Sunday, Mon–Fri → Monday); the Schedule summary and the month grid (month.js) start there
- the Schedule summary's dress part is `scheduleWear`: blazer days whenever jackets are worn, the vest only with ties
- the last workday can't be unticked and toasts why (`toggleDay`)
- holidays: Nager's regional holidays count when they cover at least half the regions the year's list names (`nagerKeep`; the UK lost New Year's Day, Easter Monday, Summer bank holiday); English-speaking countries (`HOL_EN`) use the local name
- Kuwait's holidays are worked out on the phone when the server gives none or the app isn't connected (`builtinHolidays`, `HOL_FIXED`, `HOL_HIJRI`; Islamic days from Intl's `islamic-umalqura` calendar, can be a day off the announced one); such a country isn't asked of Nager (a 404 each start)
- a country whose list can't load becomes the country anyway, with an empty list and `fetched` 0 (retried at the next start), and the message names it; the Days off summary then reads "XX: no holiday list"
- holiday and day-off dates outside this year show the year (`yearDate`); a past day off is refused, a range starting in the past is added from today (`addDayOff`)
- city search: asks in the script's language (`cityLang`: Arabic text → `language=ar`), lines that read the same get their district and repeats go (`cityList`), Enter in `#cfg-city` searches; "Use my location" is named by its coordinates (`geoName`)
- (fix round 2) the holiday list asks Nager.Date for every calendar year of its window (`holYears`, a month back to 13 months ahead): in December and January the window spans three years, and asking only the first and last left out the whole coming year (N6: Sydney planned New Year's Day 2027); `tests/nufix8.test.js`
- (fix round 2) a country picked in Days off is stored at once with no list (`fetched` 0) before its list is fetched, so a swipe away keeps it and the next start fetches it; while loading the list area is empty under "Loading the public holidays of …"
- past holidays stay days off for good (`holPast`, fix round 3, W1-8-01): the monthly refetch, another country and the loading or failed states keep the list's entries before today (and their unticks) beside the new list; "No public holidays" keeps them as `{country:'', list, skip, fetched:0}` (null only when none are past); Settings lists only the coming ones; before, a holiday that fell out of the refetched list (a month back) became a workday after the fact and was recorded as worn, "a day you didn't open the app"
- (fix round 2) `toggleDay`, `setDarkShirts` (and setup.js `setupSetDress`, `setupCancel`) save the settings before releasing the week's locks (`clearLocks`), as dress.js `setDress`: tapped then swiped away within ~100 ms, the change was lost
- (fix round 2) an empty city search clears the last results; a search in another language asks each result again by id in English (`cityEnglish`: Open-Meteo `/v1/get?id=&language=en`), so the stored name is English, with the name typed shown beside it on the button (`alt`, dropped by `setLocation`)

### `start.js`

- moving between pages animates (`pageIn`, from `show`): the page arriving eases in 28px from its tab's side (`TABS` today, wardrobe, stats, left to right) with a fade, `PAGE_MS` 320 on `MONTH_EASE`; a sub-page (add, pack, care) rises 14px; the one leaving hides at once (nothing laid out twice); the tab marker is `nav::before` sliding by `--ti` (`.tab-none` hides it); reduced motion switches instantly; `tests/tabmove.test.js`
- `show(t)` ends with `packShow(t)` (pack.js), which hides or shows `#pack`
- leaving the Wardrobe tab empties `#wardrobe` (`show()`): its grid (about 4,900 elements at 107 items) is drawn again on coming back; `.gi` has `content-visibility:auto` (`contain-intrinsic-size:auto 230px`)
- the stats, packing and care pages' ‹ (`.pback`) calls `subBack()`: `openStats`/`openPack`/`openCare` call `subOpen()` first, which notes the tab under them, its scroll, and, when Settings was open, its open categories and scrollTop; back restores them (`openSettings()` alone folds everything and scrolls to the top); opened from Today it goes back to Today
- the phone's Back on those pages and in Settings goes back one level: Settings and those pages share ONE history entry (`appHist`, `appKind` 'set' or 'sub'; `appPush`/`appDrop`); a popstate on it calls `subBack` or closes Settings like Done (`saveSettings`); opening a page from Settings hands the entry on, its ‹ hands it back (`appKeep`); left another way (the ‹, a tab, closing Settings) the entry is taken away with our own `history.back()` (`appPend`, let pass by the popstate); `tests/subback.test.js`
- the tabs have no Back entries (Back there leaves the app)
- the setup, the piece picker and What to buy take the same entry (new-user audit 2026-11-14: Back left the app from all three): `appKind` 'setup' (`setupOpen`, again in `setupGo` after a tap; `setupClose` drops it), 'pick' (`openPicker`/`closePicker`), 'shop' (`openShop`/`closeShop`); a popstate on it goes through `appSheetBack`: the setup steps back one step (on Welcome it stays open) and pushes again, the picker and What to buy close; `tests/backzoom.test.js`
- an entry asked for while our own step back is on its way, or while the address holds `#invite=` (the entry would carry it and Back would land on it), waits in `appWant` and is made by `appWanted()` when the step back lands (the popstate) or `dropInviteHash` (account.js) runs: Add after the setup's last step, the setup after "Run setup again" in Settings, the setup opened at start-up from an invitation
- iOS may skip a history entry made without a tap; the setup's entry is pushed again from `setupGo` (a tap) for that, the start-up one can't be checked off the phone
- a layer over a page borrows the page's entry instead of making a second one (fix round 2, 2026-11-15): `appBorrow(kind)` gives it the entry (or makes its own when none is held, or takes the place of an entry still waiting in `appWant`) and returns whose it was; `appGiveBack(kind, was)` hands it back when the layer closes by a tap (or drops the layer's own entry); closed by Back, the entry is gone, so the layer calls `appPush(was)` to make the page's again
- kinds 'ask' (a question, ui.js) and 'kit' (the tour, the close-up guide, the Home Screen sheet, the import sheet, kit.js) use it: Back closes the question as Cancel (`askBack`) and the sheet as ✕ (`kitClose(true)`; while an import is being written it stays and its entry is made again), and the page under them (Settings, Add, the item sheet) stays open with its entry; `tests/nufix9.test.js`
- kind 'rd' (the reading sheet, colpick.js `rdOpen` → `appBorrow('rd')`, kept in `rdHist`): Back closes it as × (`rdBack` → `rdClose(true)`, a reading under way is aborted), the item sheet or Add under it stays with its entry; a reading run again in the open sheet (Try again, Retake) keeps the one entry (the Back audit, 2026-11-15: Back closed the page under it and left the sheet up)
- "Run setup again" on its first step: Back closes it as its Cancel (`setupCancel`); a first setup still stays open there (the Back audit, 2026-11-15)
- the Back audit (2026-11-15): every layer checked in Chromium and WebKit with touch (`tests/backgesture.test.js`): Back closes it alone, × leaves no entry (no dead press), repeated open/close keeps `history.length`; in-page states (Today's folds, the picture's zoom and open swatch, Settings' categories) have no entries, so Back there leaves the app as on any tab
- kind 'nps' (the "Add without a photo" sheet, add.js `npsOpen` → `appBorrow('nps')`): Back closes it as × (`npsBack`; a changed card asks first and its entry is made again), Add stays with its entry; `show()` leaving Add calls `npsLeave` first
- `appTop`: the latest entry is start.js's, not the item sheet's (a question or the close-up guide opened over the item sheet); the item sheet's listener then leaves that Back alone, and ignores start.js's own step back (`appPend`); when it closes the item it marks the event (`e.dwItem`) so start.js's listener doesn't act on it too

### `ui.js`

- shared interface pieces (after app.js; nothing runs at load)
- `askConfirm(message, {ok, cancel, danger})` → `Promise<boolean>`: a bottom sheet (`#ask-sheet`/`#ask-backdrop`) above everything; first paragraph = question; Cancel, Escape, outside tap or a second question → false (`askAnswer`); `danger` = red button, focus on Cancel
- `tell(message)`: a toast up 4–9 s by length
- the close-up chooser's mode 'read' (`CU_COPY.read`, colpick.js `rdStart`): "Close-up for Claude" / "Claude reads the color and the weave from it. …", with the paper line
- the close-up chooser (2026-11-15, the owner's variant A from `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\closeup-chooser-mockups\`): `closeupBtn({label, cls, mode:'add'|'replace'|'retake', file:'f=>…', remove:'…', cloth})` gives a close-up button's HTML; on a touch screen (`cuTouch()`: `pointer: coarse`) a plain button calling `closeupPick`, without touch today's label around a file input (no sheet)
- `closeupPick` opens `#cu-sheet`/`#cu-backdrop` (the questions' style, z-index 2010/2011: under a question and over the colour sheet): the cloth-and-paper drawing (52 px `.wbpic`), the title and line (`CU_COPY`: "Fabric close-up" / "Replace close-up" / "Retake close-up"), Take close-up (`#cu-take`, `capture="environment"`), the paper reminder (not on Retake), Choose from photos (`#cu-lib`, no capture), Remove close-up (`#cu-rm`, quiet red, only with `remove`) and Cancel
- its two choices are labels around their file inputs, so the person's own tap opens the camera or photos (iPhone blocks a click from code); a chosen file closes the sheet and goes to the button's handler (`addTexture`, `replaceTexture`, `cnRetake`)
- Cancel, a tap outside (not in the first `ASK_SETTLE` ms), Escape (a capturing window listener ahead of the colour sheet's) and the phone's Back (`appBorrow('cu')`, start.js `appSheetBack('cu')` → `closeupBack`; `colBackFirst` stands aside while `cuOpen`) close it with nothing changed; Tab stays inside; it is in `LAYER_Q`, so the page under it is inert; `tests/closeupsheet.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit, touch and no touch)
- no `confirm()`/`alert()`/`prompt()` in the app (`tests/ui.test.js` checks); tests click `#ask-ok` / `#ask-cancel`
- `isConnected()`; `NEED_CONN` is the one "needs a connection" sentence
- `needConn(btn)` hides a server-only button's row (`.connhide`) while not connected; server-only weave reading (`claudeOnly`) is hidden too
- `.conn-only` sections hide under `body.nocon`, with a `.conn-off-note` and Connect (`goConnect`) filled by `renderConnUI`; Realistic photos shows `#photos-conn` only
- `addGate()` hides "Several items" while not connected and shows `#add-group-off`
- `applyRole()` (account.js) sets `body.owner`/`tester`/`nocon`: Advanced folds are owner-only; Connection's address and code hidden from testers
- `renderConnUI`/`connPaste`/`connSummary` draw Settings → Connection; `advToggle`/`advClose` the Advanced folds
- an ended invitation (`inviteOver()`: `invite_ended` holds the stored code) is not connected: `isConnected()` is false, so Add, Several items, item pictures and the server-only sections act as not connected; Connection's summary is "Invitation ended" and its box says so above the paste field; the Backup summary and line say nothing backs up (settings.js `backupSummary`, sync.js `showSyncStatus`); it said "Connected", "✓ … backed up" and "Backed up today" (2026-11-15)
- not connected, Connection's "Advanced · For the person who runs the server" fold is hidden until "I run the server: connect with its address and code" (`#conn-own`, `connOwn` → `body.srv-open`; styles.css `body.nocon:not(.srv-open) #adv-connection`): a new tester was invited to type into "Code"; tests click `#conn-own button` before the fold
- `plainErr(message)` turns server set-up errors (`TECH_RE`) into plain words unless the owner is connected; used by `tagFailText`, Several items, the picture batch and a tester's `setSyncStatus` (a raw OPENAI_API_KEY message once reached a new user)
- dialogs call `layerOpen`/`layerClose` (Settings, picker, What to buy); `layerInert` keeps the page behind inert and returns focus; `layerUp` traps Tab and handles Escape
- `tests/a11yfix.test.js` covers dialogs, waiting redraws, storage failures, labels, contrast, motion, tap targets
- a question takes a history entry (`appBorrow('ask')`, start.js) and the phone's Back answers it no (`askBack`), leaving the page under it open (fix round 2, 2026-11-15: Back closed Settings under "Roll back to …?" and left the question over Today)
- a real tap outside a question within `ASK_SETTLE` (450 ms) of its opening doesn't cancel it: it is the second tap of a double tap on the button that asked (`isTrusted`, so a test's `.click()` still cancels at once)

### `safety.js`

- data safety (loaded after app.js, before tagging.js and sync.js); `tests/safety.test.js`, `tests/safetysync.test.js`
- `storeFailure(e)`: marked by `txs`/`atomic` (`e.dwStore`), a `QuotaExceededError`, or an IndexedDB error event; `saveFailed` toasts it plainly (`storeFailText`) at most once per 1.5 s
- the `unhandledrejection` listener shows uncaught storage failures only; Add's `save()` relies on it and the card stays
- `dbDown`/`dbErr` come from safety.js's own `db.then`, queued before sync.js's, so the listener stays quiet when storage is down
- `storageDown(e)` (start-up chain's catch, start.js): `#nostore` over the page with the reason and Try again, the rest inert
- `askPersist()`: `navigator.storage.persist()` once (`persist_asked`, `persist_ok`), after an Add card is stored or from `safetyStart()`
- a tester's phone reports its errors by itself (2026-11-16, the owner: a tester's very first item failed and nothing showed why): `errNote(e, ctx)` notes the page too (`errWhere`) and `errSoon` sends the unsent entries of `err_log` to `POST /errors` 5 s later and at start-up (`errSend`: testers only and connected, `ERR_SEND_N` 10 a report, one report a minute, `ERR_SEND_MS`), marking them sent (`s`, kept for feedback); handled failures are noted with a context: "Photo not tagged" (`tagFailed`, not when offline), "Photo card", "Close-up reading" / "Close-up answer not applied", "Pattern reading" / "Pattern answer not applied" / "Pattern answer not drawn", "Try again", "Confirm on Add"; the worker keeps them in `errors:log` (worker/people.js `handleErrors`: 30 days, 400 at most, 20 reports a day per tester `EXTRA_CAPS.errors`, 100 for all, each one KV write), `GET /errors` is the owner's, deleting a tester deletes theirs (`dropTesterExtras`); Settings → About's feedback line tells testers (never photos or wardrobe data); read them on the PC with `node tools/read-errors.js [--name <tester>]` (DW_WORKER, DW_TOKEN); `tests/errreport.test.js`
- nothing fails silently (2026-11-14, after a tester's buttons did nothing on an iPhone): an error nobody caught (`error`, `unhandledrejection` that is not a storage failure) within `ERR_TAP_MS` (3 s) of a tap or Enter toasts "Something went wrong: … Try again; if it keeps happening, tell us in Settings → About → Feedback." (`errTell`, at most once in 5 s); every one, storage failures too, is noted in localStorage `err_log` (`errNote`: time, message, first stack line, app version; the last `ERR_MAX` 20; `errNoise` leaves out AbortError, the ResizeObserver loop warning and "Script error."); Send feedback (about.js) adds the last week's (`errReport`, at most 8) under "— Problems noted on this phone —" (`fbWithErrs`, within 4,000 characters) and clears the log once sent; `tests/errnet.test.js`
- a full WebKit run (`npm run test:webkit` with `TEST_ALL=1`, 2026-11-14) has about 50 failures that are the harness, not the app: no service worker, mouse wheel or clipboard permission in Playwright's Windows WebKit, mocked cross-origin routes refused ("access control checks"), no OffscreenCanvas WebGL in its worker, frame-timing checks, `translate(x)` serialised without its 0px, fixed waits too short for its slower planner; each app flow they cover was checked by hand there and works (the sample wardrobe and its removal, formal days, Settings colours, past days)
- `safetyStart()` (after `appReady`): `pruneTagImgs()` removes from `dw-keep-tagimg` the copies (`tagimg/<id>`, `closeimg/<id>`) of items no longer present; start-up only (undo window), never with an empty wardrobe
- tagging failures: `tagFailText` gives the Add note (`aiError`) and the reason (`tagWhy`), a 429's `message` as is; `tagFailed(q)` sets `q.tagErr`, drawn by `tagFailNote` (`.tagfail`) with Try again (`retryTag`)
- the reason (`tagReason`) is the server's `message`, then `detail`, in plain words (`plainErr`), one full stop (`oneStop`), its "Try again later, or add the item by hand." left out; a bare code ("mock", "busy") becomes "The photo couldn't be read just now"; a network failure sets `q.tagNet` (read again once online, add.js)
- `guardUnsaved()` (once, `guardOn`): `beforeunload` asks while `queue` holds an unsaved card
- `adoptBackupSettings`: on a device that never finished a sync (no `last_sync`), the backup's settings win key by key over newer local ones; device-only keys (`setupDone`) stay
- why: first-run setup stamps settings before connecting, and they replaced the backup's holidays, days off, colours and preferences

### `sync.js`

#### Sync paths
- backup/sync through the worker's `/sync` (KV); backup file download/restore
- a change syncs without the whole backup (`syncLite`): `GET /sync?lite=1&since=<sync_mark's version>` brings only records changed since, photos as `dwph:<hash>`, every tombstone and `idx` (the backup's stamp of every record)
- with nothing changed here (`sync_mark`: backup version + wardrobe fingerprint) it adds `&v=<rev>:<saved>`, so an older worker answers `same` or the whole backup and the old way runs
- photos: taken from the device's own copy by hash (`phIn`) or downloaded (`GET /sync?ph=…`, `PH_BATCH` 40); `POST /sync {baseRev, baseSaved, day, reason?, delta, photos}` sends only records newer than `idx` and photos the backup lacks (`phOut`)
- a photo is any `data:` string of `PH_MIN` (16384)+ characters anywhere in a record, hashed as the first 20 hex of its SHA-256 (`phHash`, the same in worker/backup.js); a shorter one (the swatch) stays inline in the record (2026-11-15, the owner's option A; it was 256)
- strings from `PH_OLD` (256, the rule before) may still be references in records an older app or worker saved (a PNG swatch): `phIn` matches them to this device's copy and takes them when downloaded (`isPhOld`); `tests/inlineswatch.test.js`
- measured on the owner's 16.7 MB backup: a day marked worn went from 16.7 MB each way to 15 KB down and 0.3 KB up
- missing something `idx` says the backup holds → asks for everything (`all`), so a lost wardrobe comes back; 409 → asks again; 400 `missing` → sends those photos; 422 `legacy` (over 20 MB, stored by an older app) → the old way
- the full PUT body is `{baseRev, day, reason?, data}` with `data` last (the worker reads a large backup's leading fields without parsing it)
- `window.SYNC_OLD` before load syncs the old way (`SYNC_LITE` false)
- `syncOnce` calls `adoptBackupSettings` (safety.js) before merging
- `tests/deltasync.test.js`: two devices and an older app, every kind of change under 100 KB each way, against the old worker (tests/fixtures/old-worker/worker-ef18d06.js)
- photos go up in batches (2026-11-16, the audit's W2-18-01: a restored file on a new account sent one 61 MB POST at every close, refused 413 over `BACKUP_MAX`, storing nothing): `phBatch` puts items into one POST until the next item's new photos would pass `SYNC_SEND_MAX` (16 MiB; one item always goes); days, settings and deletions go in the first batch; after a batch the next round asks `since` the version it made (`again.batch`, up to `SYNC_BATCH_MAX` 40 rounds a sync, not counted as retries); the status reads "Backing up: N items with photos still to send…" (`showSyncStatus`, Backup summary "Backing up photos"); works with the worker as it was (plain partial deltas)
- a 413 halves the budget (`syncSendMax`) and tries again; one item still too large, or a full PUT answered 413, pauses uploads until the next UTC day with `TOO_BIG_SYNC`, so nothing is resent at every close; a roll-back's first batch keeps "Before the last roll-back" and ends `sync_restore` (`restoreDone`), so later batches don't replace that copy
- `window.SYNC_SEND_TEST` before load sets a smaller budget for a test; `tests/w3fix6.test.js`
- a storage failure on this phone during a sync (full, closed) is said in the app's words in a toast (`saveFailed`) and the status ("Backup failed: your phone’s storage is full…"), not the browser's (W1-11-05); app.js `atomic` aborts with the error a `put` throws at once, not WebKit's "IDBTransaction will abort due to uncaught exception"
- the daily backups list names each copy by the day it is for, with its item count (`snapLabel`: "Mon, Nov 16, start of the day · 5 items", "… · empty", "Start of November 2026 · 2 items"; W2-14-03, W1-6-07: two "Sun, Nov 15, 7:30 AM" entries, one full, one empty); the count is the worker's `n` (copies made before have none); an empty copy's roll-back question says it empties the wardrobe
- `backedUpWords()`: what Connection (ui.js) and the setup's Connection step say about backups; while `sync_pause` it says backups are paused until the reset and the newest changes are only on this phone (W1-10-04)
- the Backup summary says "Paused until <time>" when the next 00:00 UTC is still today here, "Paused until tomorrow" otherwise (settings.js `pausedUntil`, W1-10-03)
