- in a Safari tab on an iPhone or iPad (`safariTab`: kit.js `homeIOS`, not `homeStandalone`) a backup pause adds that Download backup keeps a copy meanwhile (`SAFARI_KEEP`, W1-11-06), and the unconnected status adds why the file matters (`safariWord`)
- `downloadBackup` keeps the time in `localStorage.backup_file_at` (kit.js `safariRemind`)

#### First pull and locks
- a connected device saves nothing at start-up until a sync has merged the backup (`afterPull`, `pulledYet`, `pullDone`, `whenPulled`): Today's history and locks, arrivals, `texSwatches`, `recolourSpecSetts`, `upgradeCloseups`, `upgradePhotos`, `smallPhotos`; not connected, an ended invitation or no backup (501): at once; offline: they wait
- why: a phone two days behind locked the week and re-saved 82 items before syncing; their newer stamps replaced another device's pick and tags and brought back deleted items
- a start-up fix-up saves an item only while it is still the copy in `items` (`stillCur`, app.js), and redraws through `fixupRedraw` (never with Settings open, since `renderWardrobe` closes it)
- the week's locks are marked as the app's own (`lockOnly`, `base`; only `saveDay(rec, quiet, true)` keeps the mark): in a merge (`dayWins` in core.js `mergeState`, worker/backup.js `mergeLite`) a newer automatic copy loses to an older one changed after its base
- such a kept day is sent up by the small sync (`keptD`) and kept by the worker, so devices settle; `tests/staledevice.test.js` replays it

#### Timing, limits, errors
- a tester's phone backs up on going to the background or closing, or 15 min after the first waiting change (`TESTER_SYNC_MS`, `syncDue`); the owner's 2.5 s after a change
- over a daily backup limit nothing uploads until the next UTC day (`sync_pause`, `sync_pause_msg`), with the server's sentence; connecting again clears it
- the worker's daily KV writes used up (Cloudflare free plan: 1,000 a day, reset 00:00 UTC; the PC's picture uploads once used them up): 429 `{error:'kv-limit'}` (`kvFull`, worker.js), the same pause, and "Today's backups are used up on the server…" (`backupFullMsg`); `tests/kvlimit.test.js`
- an invitation switched off (403 `invite off`): said once (`inviteEnded`, `localStorage.invite_ended`) and syncing stops
- nothing more is asked with an ended code: photos.js `workerBase()` is null while `inviteOver()` (the photo list, item and outfit pictures, the masters, holiday lists), app.js `ask()` sends no photo, tagging.js `claudePattern` and add.js `addMulti`/`fetchPhoto` don't call; a photo answered 403 'invite off' marks the invitation ended (`endedAnswer` → `inviteEnded`); the first open after an end still sends what runs before the sync learns of it (re-audit 2026-11-15: 12–45 refused requests per open)
- the one-time message of an ended invitation (`endedTold`): a replaced link says the new one opens the same wardrobe (`ENDED_NEW`), a deleted account says the backup was deleted (`ENDED_GONE`, W1-6-03), else `ENDED`; status lines keep `ENDED`
- `endedAnswer(status, j)`: 403 'invite off', or 403 'forbidden token' on a tester's phone (the account was deleted on another phone or by the owner), is an ended invitation for the sync, the daily backups list, Feedback and `loadAccount`; it said "Backups aren’t available on this server yet" and kept trying (2026-11-15)
- sync errors show the server's `message` before its `error`

#### The phone's clock (fix round 3, 2026-11-16, the audit's W1-9-01..08, W1-5-07, W2-13-03)
- every answer of the worker carries its time (`x-now`, exposed by CORS); each sync's GET and save learns the gap (`clockLearn`: the server's time less the request's middle; under `CLOCK_MIN` 3 s it is 0) into `localStorage.clock_skew` and core.js `clockSkew`
- `nowMs()` (core.js) is the phone's time corrected by that gap; every stamp uses it: items' `updated`/`added` (app.js `put`/`del`, add.js, kit.js, shop.js), days (history.js `saveDay`, the week's locks and `lockAt`), settings (`stampSettings`), holidays' `fetched`, the roll-back, the tombstone cutoff
- a new stamp is always above the one it replaces (`nextStamp(prev)`): an item's edit, its deletion mark (`del` returns it, the Wardrobe's Undo goes one above), a day, a setting's key; so an edit or a deletion made on a copy wins over that copy whatever the clocks say, also before any server has answered
- a gap of an hour or more is said once (`clockTell`, `clock_told`): "Your phone’s clock is 1 year ahead. Your changes are dated by the right time anyway. To fix the clock, turn on Set Automatically…"
- once a sync has learned the gap, stamps on the phone over `CLOCK_AHEAD` (10 min) past the corrected time are put back to now (`clockFix`, before the merge; the sync then asks again): items, days (`updated`, `base`, `lockAt`), tombstones, settings' `ks`/`updated`; a holiday list `fetched` ahead is fetched again (0)
- dates by the corrected time: past days are recorded only before `trueToday()` (history.js `recordDays`), no day is recorded for today and no week locked while the phone's date is ahead of it (`lockUpcoming`), the holiday window (`fetchHolidays`), a list fetched ahead is due (`refreshHolidays`), the backup pause (`nextUtcDay`, `syncPaused`: a pause over 26 h away is let go), the daily copy's `day`
- a roll-back stamps each restored item above this phone's deletion mark and copy of it, each day above its copy, and the settings above every key (`rollBackState`)
- measured KV writes (lab, a tester phone; before → after): setup day 4 → 4, a light day 2 → 2, an Add day (3 pieces) 2 → 2; `tests/clock.test.js`

#### Daily backups
- before the first PUT of each day the worker keeps the backup as `snap:<YYYY-MM-DD>` (30 days) and `snap:<YYYY-MM>` (400 days); `GET /sync?snaps=1` lists, `?snap=<id>` returns one
- "Roll back" (`loadSnapshots`/`rollBack`/`rollBackState`) restamps the snapshot's records and tombstones the rest, so it wins on every device; the PUT carries `reason:'restore'` and the worker keeps `snap:before-restore`
- a roll-back's upload is marked in localStorage before the phone's copy changes (`sync_restore` = the code; `restoreWanted`/`restoreDone`): every upload until one goes through carries `reason:'restore'`, so a kill or reload mid-roll-back still gets "Before the last roll-back" kept at the next start (2026-11-15, the new-user audit: it was memory only and 24 pieces were lost for good); `tests/rollback.test.js`
- what a roll-back says (`rollSay`) goes to Settings' line and, when Settings isn't open (closed under its question by Back, or the toast's Undo), to a toast too: a success has Undo, which asks and rolls back to "Before the last roll-back" (`rollBack({id, label})`); written only to the closed Settings page the wardrobe vanished from Today with no word (re-audit 2026-11-15); `tests/rollbackwhere.test.js`
- a copy from before the setup (`snapPreSetup`: no `setupDone`, no items, and this phone's setup done) brings back no settings: the setup started again at Welcome with the workdays and location gone; the message says the settings were kept
- the daily backups list is read when Settings' Backup opens (`setCatOpen` in settings.js → `loadSnapshots`), not only on the dropdown's focus (an iPhone opens the picker at the tap, with only "Choose a day…"); once per page life (each list is a counted server call), one request at a time (`snapLoading`), the chosen day kept
- the list cap (429 `what:'list'`) is said as `SNAP_CAP` ("The daily backups can’t be listed again today…"), not the server's "photo list checks"
- settings a synced copy marks removed (`kh` '-': a roll-back, a removal elsewhere) leave memory too (`takeSettings` in core.js, used wherever stored or downloaded settings are taken: start.js, safety.js, settings.js): a roll-back's removed day off and holidays stayed in memory and the next change stamped them back on every phone

#### Messages
- a sync that brings changes while Settings is open redraws its controls (`syncSettingsRedraw`: `renderSettingsExtras`; while a text field in it is focused, once it is left)
- a backup that never reached the server says so in words (`syncNetText`: "Not backed up: you’re offline…" / "…the server couldn’t be reached…", `syncWaiting`), with the last backup's time; the `online` event backs up again; `netErr` (ui.js) turns "Load failed" / "Failed to fetch" into plain words inside `plainErr` for everyone (picture batch, Several items, roll-back and backup lists)
- the Backup summary (`backupSummary`, kept current by `showSyncStatus`): "Paused until tomorrow" while `sync_pause`, "Waiting for a connection", "Not backed up: ask for a new invitation link", and "Backed up today · newer changes waiting" while `sync_wait` (set by `scheduleSync`, cleared by `synced`)
- a tester's code the server no longer knows (403 `forbidden token`: storage lost, account deleted) ends the invitation (`endedAnswer`, `inviteEnded(token, answer)`) and is also marked `localStorage.invite_lost`: a new invitation link then asks "Back up this phone’s wardrobe…" and keeps the wardrobe instead of "Clear and switch" (account.js `connectFromLink`; 2026-11-15)
- the write ceiling is worded by the app (`backupFullMsg`: `pr.write`, `pr.all` → "everyone’s backups"), without the server's photo count
- feedback refused shows the server's `message` (or an `error` that is a sentence), never a code like `kv-limit` (about.js `sendFeedback`)

#### Redraws
- a sync's redraw waits while the item sheet or piece picker is open (`syncRedraw` → `syncLater`; `syncLaterRun` on `itemShown(false)` or `closePicker`)
- the monthly holiday refetch (`refreshHolidays`, settings.js) syncs first and refetches only if still needed, never offline: its newest stamp undid a newer change from elsewhere
- the Backup line (`backupSummary`, settings.js; `showSyncStatus`) shows the last backup, a failure, or "Not backed up yet", and keeps the last backup while the next runs ("… · syncing…")

#### Field-by-field merge and deletions elsewhere (fix round 3, 2026-11-16)
- `applyMerged` checks deletions with core.js `tombWins` and tells the phone when an edit it made after a piece was deleted elsewhere wasn't kept ("“X” was deleted on another phone, so your change to it wasn’t kept."); with the item sheet open it calls itemsheet.js `itemSynced` with the copy shown before
- `syncLite`'s "lacks" check doesn't count an item the backup sent that a deletion here outweighs (`dead`): an older worker keeps it by its stamp alone, and the app would ask for everything at every sync
- the owner's "Delete everything" on another phone (W1-6-01): the worker keeps `wiped` {at} (people.js `handleAccount`, `WIPED_KEY`) and `GET /sync?lite=1` answers `wiped` for the owner; `syncWiped`: a phone whose `last_sync` is before it merges and sends nothing, says `WIPED_SYNC` ("Backups held: …"; the Backup summary "Held: deleted from the server on another device") and asks once "Back it up again" (localStorage `wipe_seen` = that time, then it syncs: everything goes up) / "Not now" (nothing sent, asked again only from Sync now, `wipeLater`); a phone that never synced, or synced after it, goes on as usual; a tester's other phones stop as before (the invitation is gone)
- Restore file: the file's settings are spread over this phone's and all stamped now (`stampAllSettings`), so they win (W1-11-03: the new setup's vest choice and holiday country won the per-setting merge after a wipe); items and days merge as usual
#### Records from outside (fix round 3)
- `restoreBackup`, `syncLite` (after `phIn`), the whole sync and `rollBack` run core.js `cleanState` on what came in; `syncLite` and the whole sync run it on what they send (the delta only, in the small sync)
- Restore file says how many items it left out ("Restored. Your wardrobe now has 6 items. 2 items in the file weren’t a Daily Wardrobe item and were left out.")
- a record the check left out of a sync is remembered per server address (localStorage `sync_off` {url, i, d}, 500 each) so `syncLite`'s "lacks" test doesn't ask for the whole backup at every sync because the server still holds it
- app.js `cleanStored` (start-up, once per phone: `rec_clean` '1', set once every write went through): a stored record the check changes is written back with its stamp unchanged (no upload; other phones clean their own), one with no usable id or type is taken out of storage
- nothing is dropped silently: before any stored record is changed or taken out, every original is added to meta `rec_kept` ([{kind, dropped, rec, at}], appended, never replaced), and a one-time toast says so (`recKeptTell`: "N items in this phone’s storage couldn’t be shown safely and were set aside. … Nothing was deleted: the originals are kept on this phone.")
- per-field stamps (`item.fs`, `day.fs`: field or slot → stamp) pass the check unchanged (plain words as keys, finite numbers), and settings (`ks`/`kh`, `kse`/`khe`) are never touched by it; the server's `recCheck` takes them too; `tests/records.test.js`
- weave, patternSpec, tagAI, meas and away keep their prose as it is, quotes included (core.js `recProse`), only `< > \`` taken out, and a value under a `hex` key must be a hex color (patternSpec.across bands reach a fill); worker/records.js `proseOk` is the same rule
- checked 2026-11-16 over 6,007 stored-shape items and 51,012 days in the audits' data (lab KV stores with photos as references, the 12 Nov build's copies, the 14 Nov new-user backup with own colors, backups with photos inline, 590 sample pieces) and in a browser start-up pass over 102 items (sample saved the app's way with sampleMine, own colors and patterns with digits, close-ups, band/motif/texture readings, patternSpec and weave prose with quotes, a picture record): the check changed nothing but the crafted records (`C:\code\w3-fix1-lab\realdata.js`, `startpass.js`)
- the second lock, at the markup: ids inside inline handlers go through `jsq` (photos.js: JS-escaped, then `esc`), ids in attributes through `esc`, item and picture sources through `srcOk` (a data:image base64 picture, a blob: or http(s) address or a file path with nothing that ends the attribute, else empty), in wardrobe.js, itemsheet.js, today.js, photos.js, add.js, history.js, care.js, colpick.js, stats.js and kit.js
- photos.js `keepRender` keeps a picture record only when its `img` is a whole `REC_PHOTO` string (W2-13-06); assemble.js `asmPre` takes a server picture only of that shape
- tagging.js `confTxt` shows only the words medium and low (W2-13-05); item names typed or imported lose bidi override and isolate characters on save (wardrobe.js `setItemField`, `wardSaveFields`), and the owner's Feedback received shows feedback without them (about.js) (W2-12-06)
- checked for backup data reaching markup (2026-11-16): Today (pieces, tiles, swatches, the picture), the Wardrobe grid and item sheet, the piece picker, Insights, What to buy, the month, packing, Laundry, Settings' lists and the pictures; names and other free text were already escaped everywhere, the planner's words (`desc`, `nm`) are built from keys only

### `account.js`

- who the app is connected as (after sync.js); `acct` holds `GET /me`
- invite link `<app url>#invite=<code>&w=<server>`; `connectFromLink` stores `worker_url`/`app_token` only when `/me` accepts; `acctStart()` runs it for `#invite=` at start-up
- a refused link changes nothing; `#invite` is dropped from the address unless the server was unreachable
- a link on a phone connected with another code: with a local wardrobe (`acctLocal`, read from the database) it asks, wipes (`wipeDevice`) and reloads; else it switches and drops `sync_mark`, `last_sync`, `sync_status` (a sync once merged the owner's wardrobe into a tester's)
- Connection's `#acct-box`: `renderAccountUI`, `loadAccount` (at most once in 3 s); testers see use against caps (`ACCT_CAPS`: item pictures, photo readings, pattern readings; `acctUse`), the owner invites (`createInvite`, `copyInvite`, `revokeInvite`, `purgeInvite`)
- `acctStart()` wraps `renderSettingsExtras` (settings.js); an old server (404/405 on `/me`) leaves the box empty
- the role is remembered per code (`rememberRole`, `acct_role`, `acctRole()`, `isTester()`)
- iPhone: a Home Screen app has its own storage; a tester connecting in Safari (`iosInSafari`) gets `homeScreenGuide()` and "Copy my invitation link" (`copyInviteLink`, `myInviteLink`; testers only)
- a daily limit shows the server's `message`: through `detail` on Add, thrown by `renderItemPic`, toasted for pattern reading via `limitNote`
- testers see no costs (`showCost()` in settings.js)
- `ACCT_CAPS` includes "Colour & pattern lookups" (`describe`, 2026-11-15)
- About's Feedback line (`#fb-help`) says where feedback goes to testers only
- tests: `tests/accounts.test.js`, `tests/testerview.test.js`, `tests/invitelink.test.js`
- `connectFromLink` decides only after storage is read (2026-11-15, the new-user audit's Critical): it awaits `connRestored` and counts the wardrobe in the database (`acctLocal`: items, day records, sample pieces); at start-up the link was checked before `items` was loaded, the phone looked empty, it switched without asking and the next sync copied its wardrobe into the other account; `acctHasLocal` is gone; `tests/invitefix.test.js` holds the database's first reads back 1.5 s to reproduce it
- one link at a time (`acctLinkRun`); `opt.address` (the page's own address) says the outcome in a toast, the paste fields (Settings `connPaste`, setup `setupConnect`) say it in their own line from `acctWhy` ('bad', 'refused' with `acctWhyMsg`, 'offline', 'kept') via `acctFailText()`: offline is no longer "check that you copied all of it"
- a `#invite=` link that can't be read says "That invitation link didn’t work…" (`INVITE_BAD`); a full stop, comma or bracket after the link is ignored (`inviteParts`)
- a link opened or pasted in the tab already showing the app is read (`hashchange`); a link the server couldn't be reached for stays in the address and is tried again on `online` and on coming to the front (`acctRetryLink`); Settings' Connection and the setup's Connection step say so, with the link in the field
- while a link from the address is checked (`inviteChecking`) Connection, its summary and the setup's Connection and All set steps say "Checking your invitation…"; `acctLinkDrawn()` redraws them, Settings' summaries and Add when the check ends
- connecting (not switching) with sample pieces on the phone asks nothing and keeps them (re-audit 2026-11-15): the sample is never backed up (sync.js `syncShare`), so it can't reach the person's wardrobe or his other phones; it used to ask "Remove the sample wardrobe first?"
- (fix round 2) the Home Screen sheet after a link (`homeScreenGuide`) shows once per phone (`homeTold`, `localStorage.home_told`) and never when the phone was already connected with that code: it covered the app every time a tester tapped his link; About's button still opens it
- a successful link clears `invite_ended`; `loadAccount` marks an ended invitation (`endedAnswer`, sync.js) instead of showing "The server didn’t accept this connection" to a tester; `acctEndedUI()` redraws what said Connected
- (re-audit 2026-11-15, N1) a new link on a phone whose code has ended, switched off (`invite_ended`) as well as lost (`invite_lost`), asks "Back up this phone’s wardrobe with …’s invitation?" ("Keep it and back it up") and keeps the wardrobe, changes since the end included, instead of "Clear and switch": the old backup can't be reached, and after a switch-off the app stops syncing so it never hears of a later deletion; `tests/inviteswitch.test.js`
- "Clear and switch" first backs up what no backup took (`acctPending`: `syncMark().sig` against the wardrobe's `stateSig`, then `syncNow()`); still pending (backups paused, server refusing), the question says the changes would be lost and its button reads "Clear, losing those changes" (`acctSwitchText`); it also says the settings go and the setup starts again
- (fix round 3, W2-13-01/W2-13-04) a link is followed only to the app's own server (`acctOwnServer`): `APP_SERVERS` (the published worker's origin; someone hosting the app elsewhere adds theirs), the origin this phone is already connected to, and, only for a page served from this computer, `*.test` and loopback on the page's port + 1 (the tests and the audit's lab); any other `w=` is refused before anything is asked or sent ("…a server this app doesn’t use…", `INVITE_STRANGER`); a `w=` over `INVITE_W_MAX` (300) characters or with a name/password is unreadable (W2-13-11)
- (fix round 3, W1-7-01) before a switch the server is asked about the old code (`GET /me` with it): any refusal (switched off, replaced, deleted) is an ended invitation (the N1 "Keep it and back it up" question), not only what `invite_ended`/`invite_lost` remembered; unreachable falls back to those
- the same account under a new code (`acctIdFor`: `rememberRole` now keeps the account's `id` in `acct_role`, or the old code's `/me`) switches with no question and nothing cleared, sync marks kept, toast "…: the same wardrobe as before" (the owner's new link for a tester, decision 1)
- an empty phone connected with another code asks "Switch this phone to …’s invitation?" naming its connection (`acctSwitchEmptyText`, W2-13-04); a phone not connected with pieces of its own (sample not counted) asks "Add this phone’s N pieces to …’s wardrobe?" ("Add and connect"; W1-7-03, W1-11-01)
- the switch question for the owner's phone (old `/me` role owner) says how to come back (Settings → Connection → "I run the server", `acctComeBack`), and says the setup starts again only if the other account has no wardrobe (W1-7-04); after the switch `localStorage.acct_was_owner` keeps "I run the server" in Connection on that tester-connected phone (ui.js `renderConnUI`, styles.css `body.tester:not(.srv-open) #adv-connection`; W1-7-02)
- another address or code typed under Advanced while connected goes through the link's questions (`acctTyped` from settings.js `saveSettings`), so a typed code never merges this phone's wardrobe into another account silently
- the owner's list has "New link" on each active tester (`reissueInvite`, POST /invites {reissue: id}): a new link that opens the same wardrobe, shown once ("New link for …"); the old link stops working
- the tester's "Copy my invitation link" line says to keep the link somewhere safe: it brings the wardrobe back after the phone's data is cleared (W1-11-02)
- tests: `tests/linkfix.test.js`
- the backup before a switch (`acctBackUpFirst`) runs up to `ACCT_SYNC_TRIES` (3) times while the server takes it, since its first pull lets the start-up writers save the day's record, and gives up after `ACCT_SYNC_MS` (20 s), so a slow connection still gets the question

### `setup.js`

- first-run setup and empty states (loaded last): `#setup`, built by `setupEl()`, not in index.html
- `setupStart()` (start.js chain) opens it for an empty wardrobe with no recorded day and no `settings.setupDone`
- with items it never shows: `setupDone` is set in memory only and stored with the next settings change (storing it at once would force a full sync)
- steps `SETUP_STEPS` (welcome, week, wear, weather, connect, done) via `setupStep`, `setupGo`, `setupDraw`; Skip finishes at once
- steps store through the app's own functions (`toggleDay`, `setUnits`, `setVestMode`, `setLocation`, `useMyLocation`, `findCity`)
- `setupSearch` uses Settings' `#cfg-city` and `window._cities`; `setupLocate` polls `settings.location`
- leaving the Weather step with an unfinished city search warns once (`setupCityOpen`, `cityWarned`)
- What you wear: `settings.dress = {jacket, tie}` (`setupSetDress`, `setupDress`; missing = true), releasing the week's locks (`clearLocks`)
- a new person who doesn't choose gets `vestMode='never'` (`setupFinish`, self-opened setup only); the stored default stays `auto`
- Connection: `setupConnected`, else an invite link field (`connectFromLink`, or `setupParseLink`)
- (fix round 3, W1-11-01) not connected, the Welcome (`#su-before`) and the Connection step say an invitation link opened again brings the backed-up wardrobe back
- `setupFinish` stores `setupDone` via `saveUserSettings`; the last step lists workdays Sunday first and asks for trousers only with jackets on and no suit
- picks the time zone country's holidays (`setupHolidays`, `tzCountry`, `TZ_CC`); without a list yet the country is kept with `fetched` 0 and `refreshHolidays` retries
- default workdays Sunday to Thursday (`settings.workdays` [0..4]): the owner's choice
- "Run setup again" (`#setup-again`) → `setupOpen(true)`, keeping the stored vest choice
- a self-opened setup steps aside when items arrive or another tab opens (`setupYield`, from `show()` and `setupToday`)
- while up the page is inert (`setupInert`); z-index 980, under Settings
- under `navigator.webdriver` it opens only with `window.SETUP_AUTO=true` (`setupAuto`), since every test starts as a new install
- Today's checklist (`setupToday`, rows from `setupNeeds`): suit or blazer, shirt, tie, trousers ("If no suit" with jackets on and no suit), shoes; goes in place of the picture, above the score card or under the note depending on how dressed the day is
- empty Wardrobe: `setupWardEmpty()` (`#wempty`)
- `tests/setup.test.js` covers the flow, both connection paths, stepping aside, both checklist kinds, and layout at 320/390/626px
- (audit 2026-11-15) the step on screen is kept in `localStorage.setup_at` (`setupKeep`/`setupResume`: `{k, again, before, vestSet}`), so a setup iOS cut off opens again on that step; removed by `setupClose`
- the new-person "No vest" applies only when the vest has no stamp (`setupVestChosen`: `setStampOf(settings,'vestMode')`), so a choice made before a kill, or the account's from another phone, stays
- the vest row shows only with jackets and ties (as Settings); the dress switches keep other `settings.dress` keys (jeans)
- "Run setup again" has Cancel instead of Skip (`setupCancel`): it puts back `SETUP_UNDO` (workdays, blazer days, dress, vest, units, location) as they were at open (`setup.before`, kept with the step)
- a tap at the spot of the tap that closed the setup within `SETUP_TAP_MS` (350) is dropped (`setupTapGuard`): a double tap on Skip opened Settings, one on "Add my first item" skipped the tour's first card; tests wait past it
- connected at the setup's own Connection step (`setup.joined`, or while `setup.busy`), items arriving don't close it: it goes on to All set with the account's answers (adoptBackupSettings) and says the wardrobe came across
- All set lists the blazer days and the vest; its "Try a sample wardrobe" (`setupSample`) finishes the setup first (holidays, vest)
- `setupOpen` closes a Home Screen kit sheet (one explanation)
- `tests/setupfix.test.js` covers these and the Settings fixes of the same audit
- (fix round 2) a cut-off "Run setup again" (`setup_at` with `again`) opens again at start-up whatever `setupNo` says (start-up's `show('today')` sets it whenever there are items, so it never came back for anyone with a wardrobe); one that can't be opened then (no `setupDone`, Settings open, a test without `SETUP_AUTO`) is dropped, so it never opens by itself later with a Cancel that would undo settings changed since
- (fix round 2) a double tap on Get started / Next / Back steps once (`setupGoDouble`: a real tap within `SETUP_TAP_MS` at the last step tap's spot is dropped; calls from code always step); tests wait 400 ms between taps on the same button
- (fix round 2) the first setup's finish stores the time zone's country (`setupHolCountry`: list empty, `fetched` 0) with `setupDone`, before `setupHolidays` fetches it; `setupHolidays` also fetches for a country kept with no list; swiped away right after finishing, the next start's `refreshHolidays` fetches it
- (fix round 2) the setup's city search clears its results on an empty search and shows a non-English find's English name with the typed one beside it
- an ended invitation: the Connection step says so ("Your invitation has ended, so the link you used no longer works…") and All set reads "invitation ended"

### `kit.js`

- getting started (2026-11-14, the owner's ask after the audit's new-user walkthrough), loaded after setup.js; `tests/kit.test.js`
- Safari-only users (2026-11-16, the owner's decision 2 for W1-11-04): the Home Screen step (setup and About's sheet) says once, when not connected, that Safari can clear the app's data if it isn't opened for about a week or runs short of space, and that the Home Screen app and Download backup protect it (`homeRisk`)
- `safariRemind` (from safety.js `safetyStart`, 5 s after start): an iPhone or iPad Safari tab, not connected, `SAFARI_MIN` (10) or more own items, never backed up (no `last_sync`, no `backup_file_at`): a toast with Download backup, at most once every 30 days (`SAFARI_EVERY`, `localStorage.safari_remind`), not over another toast or the setup
- one full-screen sheet (`#kitsheet`, z-index 2000, the page inert while open, Escape closes, ← → step) serves the tour, the close-up guide and the import (`kitOpen`/`kitDraw`/`kitClose`/`kitGo`)
- the sheet takes a history entry or borrows Add's (`kitHist` → start.js `appBorrow('kit')`, kept in `kit.hist`): the phone's Back closes it like ✕, and Add stays under the import sheet (fix round 2, 2026-11-15: Back left the app from the tour and the Home Screen sheet, and closed Add under the import sheet)
- the import sheet's paste field scrolls into view under the header when it is focused (`kitPasteShow`, 350 ms later, for the keyboard)
- the quick tour (`tourOpen(then, lastLabel)`, `TOUR_CARDS`): four cards with drawn pictures: the score, the close-up (white paper along one edge), Shade, patterns; marked seen on close (`localStorage.tour_seen`, `tourSeen`)
- a first-time person who taps "Add my first item" at the setup's end gets the tour between the setup and Add, its last button "Add my first item" (`setupFinish`); Escape or ✕ also goes on to Add
- Add to Home Screen names the browser it is open in (2026-11-16, a tester in Chrome on an iPhone was shown Safari's steps): `homeBrowser()` (kit.js: CriOS Chrome, FxiOS Firefox, EdgiOS Edge, else Safari; `window.DW_BROWSER` in tests) picks step 1 (`homeShareStep`: Chrome's Share at the right end of its address bar, else its ⋯ menu; Firefox and Edge their menu's Share; Safari as before) and step 2's hint (Edit Actions, Safari's View More); `homeNote`, Settings → Connection's line (account.js) and the reminder line (remind.js, `homeShareShort`) say the browser's name; every iOS browser draws with Safari's engine and a Home Screen app added from any of them keeps its own storage; `tests/homechrome.test.js`
- the tour is offered again by "Take the 1-minute tour" on the setup's last step and Today's first-outfit checklist (`kitStartRow`), and Settings → About → "Take the tour" (`#tour-again`)
- the close-up guide (`kitCloseupGuide`, `GUIDE_CARD`): fill the screen with cloth, plain white paper along one edge out of the dashed middle (where the colour is measured), the same light on both, the paper white not blown out, tap the paper; opened from the Add card's close-up step ("Tips for a good close-up", `.texguide` in `texSlot`) and the tour's close-up card; its pictures draw the paper as a straight band
- the Add card's close-up step itself says "Lay white paper along one edge and tap it on the screen to set the exposure." (`.texpaper` in add.js `texSlot`, the owner's ask), so a returning user sees it without opening the guide
- Undo after Confirm (`kitSaved`, called at the end of add.js `save`): "Saved: <name>." with Undo for `KIT_UNDO_MS` (8 s); cards saved within 1.5 s of each other are one Undo ("Saved 2 items")
- How many (add.js `qtyRow`/`stepQty`/`qtyOf`, 2026-11-14, the owner: "tap a plus or minus for the amount before I add", shirts and socks only, `QTY_TYPES`): a "How many" stepper above Confirm, 1 to `QTY_MAX` (12), 44 px buttons, changed in place (no redraw); Confirm reads "Confirm 3" and `save` makes that many items, each its own id (deep copies of the first: same tags, photo and close-up; the server keeps a photo once by its hash), so the planner and laundry count them apart; the toast "Saved 3: White." and its Undo take them all back (`kitSaved(it,q,i,copies)`); another type ignores a number set before; `tests/addqty.test.js`
- `kitQuiet` silences Undo while it is set (nothing sets it since the import and the sample stopped going through `save`)
- `kitUnsave` deletes the items (tombstones, as Delete) and puts their cards back on Add in their places; `q.raw` and the canvases were already dropped by save, so white balance can't be toggled again on a restored card
- Duplicate (`kitDuplicate`, `#iv-dup` in itemsheet.js): a deep copy with a new id and `added` now, without `paused`, `order`, `arrives`, `away`, `cleaned`, `sample` (`KIT_NO_COPY`); opens the copy
- Duplicate is its own row (`.ivdup`) above Delete, which stays alone at the very end of the item sheet (`tests/wardrobetap.test.js`, `tests/uipages.test.js`)
- import from a spreadsheet (`kitImportOpen`: Add → "Import from a spreadsheet" `#add-import`, and the empty Wardrobe card): a CSV/TSV file or pasted rows (`kitSplit`: tab, ; or , from the first row, quotes)
- the first row names the columns in any order (`KIT_COLS`: Type and Colour required; second/third colour, pattern, name, brand, material, notes, vest, cuffs, kind, style, leather, season)
- words are matched to the app's lists (`KIT_TYPE_WORDS`; `kitColour`: colour names as shown, the owner's own colours by name; `kitPattern` with synonyms)
- a preview row per line says what was left out and why (left-out rows first; "Type is empty", "Colour is empty", an unknown type lists what the app plans, an unknown colour lists the type's colours); nothing is saved until "Add N items"; "Download a template" (`KIT_TEMPLATE`, a `data:` link: a blob revoked a second later can fail on an iPhone)
- many items at once are written in ONE IndexedDB transaction (`kitTx`, `kitStore`, `kitDelMany` with their tombstones and day records; 2026-11-15, the new-user audit): the import, the sample, their Undo and removal store all or nothing on a kill, and a double tap is ignored (`kitImpBusy`, `kitSampling`); items are built as add.js `saveCard` builds a hand-added card (`kitItem`), not through `save`; a write that throws part-way aborts the transaction
- import words: plurals and everyday type names (`kitType`: Blazers, Watches, Dress shirts), everyday colours through core.js `COL_ALIAS` and "<colour> grey" (`kitColour`, said as "read as …"), a colour the type isn't offered in moved by `fitCol` (said), materials matched to `MATERIALS_BY` in any case and shoes'/belts' Material as their leather (`kitMaterial`), a "Kind" column, Striped/Check/Repp/Bengal stripe as patterns
- import materials (2026-11-15, the materials audit): another word for a listed material is read as it (core.js `matAlias`: Merino, Fresco, Broadcloth, Lisle, Wool-linen, Wool-silk, Camel, Nylon/spandex…, said "read as"); a shirt's weave in the Material column (oxford, twill, end-on-end…, `SHIRT_WEAVE_MAT`) becomes its pattern when the row's is plain (said), else is left out (said); any other word is kept as typed (Other)
- shoes' and belts' leather from the Material or Leather column (`kitLeather`): "Pebble grain" / "Scotch grain" with a space are the grains (they fell through to "leather" and saved as calf), Nubuck is nubuck where the style takes it, else suede (said), "braided" a braided belt, "leather"/"calf" calf
- import files are decoded by `kitDecode` (UTF-8, UTF-16 by its mark, else Windows-1252); an empty file says so
- import preview rows have a tick (`kitImpTick`); a row matching a piece already in the wardrobe (type, colours, pattern, name: `kitDupKey`) starts unticked as "already in your wardrobe" (`kitDupMark`); "Add N items" shows "Adding k of n…" over 40 rows, Cancel and ✕ wait for it
- the import's Undo lasts `KIT_IMP_UNDO_MS` (20 s); afterwards the import sheet offers "Take them out" for the last import (`KIT_IMP_LAST` in localStorage, `kitUnimport`)
- the sample wardrobe (`kitSampleLoad`, `KIT_SAMPLE`: 24 pieces, three suits, two blazers, two trousers, five shirts, five ties, shoes, belts, socks, a watch): saved with `item.sample`, asks first when the wardrobe has own pieces and no sample, jumps to the next workday (`goToday`); offered on the setup's last step, Today's checklist and the empty Wardrobe
- the sample follows the dress code (`kitSampleCards`: no suits/blazers without jackets, then `KIT_SAMPLE_NOJKT` two more trousers; no ties without ties); a tap adds only the pieces missing (`kitSampleSig`: type, colours, pattern, cuffs), so a partial sample from before is completed, never doubled
- the sample from the setup's last step finishes the setup first (`kitSampleDone` → `setupFinish(false)`: holidays, the no-vest default)
- a sample piece the owner changed (`kitEdited`: `item.sampleMine`, set by an edit in the item sheet) or kept ("Keep it as mine" in the item sheet, `kitKeepSample`, `kitSampleNote`) is his; the grid tags the others "Sample" (`kitSampleTag`, wardrobe.js); item pictures leave sample pieces out (photos.js `missingItemPics`)
- the Wardrobe grid's eyebrow names chinos and jeans ("Chinos"), not "Trousers"
- Add to Home Screen (2026-11-14, the owner: show a new iPhone user how to add it as a web app): on an iPhone or iPad in a browser tab (`homeNeeded` in kit.js: `homeIOS` and not `homeStandalone`; `window.DW_IOS` / `DW_STANDALONE` for tests) the setup's steps are worked out when it opens (`SETUP_STEPS = setupStepsNow()`, from `SETUP_BASE`) with a Home Screen step right after Welcome (`homeSteps(true)`): the reason (full screen, the only way iOS lets it send the morning reminder), three taps with the Share and Add icons (Share at the bottom of Safari, at the top on an iPad), and "do this before you set anything up": the Home Screen app keeps its own storage, apart from Safari's; Next carries on in Safari; Settings → About's "Add to Home Screen" (`#home-again`, `homeOpen`, the same instructions as a kit sheet) shows only there (`homeAbout`, set once at start; `#home-again[hidden]{display:none}`: the Settings buttons' display rule outranked the attribute); elsewhere the setup is as before (6 steps); `tests/homescreen.test.js`
- Android install (2026-11-14, the owner: "the same thing for android with an install button"): on Android in a browser tab (`homeAndroid`, `window.DW_ANDROID` for tests) the setup's step after Welcome is "Install the app" (`homeStepsAndroid`; `SETUP_TITLES.home` is a getter): the browser's own offer is held (`beforeinstallprompt` → `homeEvt`, its banner prevented) and an Install button (`.home-install`, `homeInstall`: `prompt()` once, then the offer is gone) shows in place of the menu steps (`.home-manual`: ⋮ → Install app / Add to Home screen) only while there is an offer, updated in place (`homeSync`; the offer can come after the step is drawn); `appinstalled` toasts; no "set up first" warning (the installed app shares the browser's storage); About's button reads "Install the app" (`homeLabel`); `[hidden]` on them needs `display:none!important` (the setup's button rule outranks it); `tests/homescreen.test.js` fakes the offer
- (audit 2026-11-15, H12) one Home Screen explanation (`homeSteps`, iPhone): ⋯ then Share (Share at the top right on an iPad), Add to Home Screen (or View More), Open as Web App on, Add, open it; a connected tester gets "Copy my invitation link" (`homeCopy`, `homeTester`) and is told to paste it at the Home Screen app's Connection step; the note (`homeNote`) says what applies: connected, not connected with a wardrobe (it stays in Safari: Download backup / Restore file), or not connected and empty (set it up there)
- account.js's `homeScreenGuide` opens that sheet (never over the setup); About's buttons call `tourAgain`/`homeAgain`, which go back to Settings → About on close
- the tour's score card speaks of shirts and trousers without ties or jackets (`kitWears`)
- Today shows "You're trying a sample wardrobe" (`kitSampleBanner`, `#kit-sample`) with Add my own and Remove the sample (`kitSampleRemove`: asks, says "Removing the sample…" at once, deletes every sample piece he didn't change and takes them out of every day record that names one (`kitSampleDay`), in one transaction, releases locks); `tests/kitfix.test.js`
- `kitSampleDay` (re-audit 2026-11-15, N2: cutting a record to its occasion let history.js `recordDays` refill the past day from today's plan: "Didn't" days became worn, a suit bought later was worn before it came): a past day (or one marked worn) keeps everything (worn, `edited`, occasion, his own pieces) with the sample's slots null and marked in `rec.gone` (planner.js `frozenOutfit` rates it without them, as after a Delete)
- a past day that wore only sample pieces becomes `rec.noOutfit` (no `ids`): `recordDays` leaves it, `planSim` skips it (no outfit, no rest or variety from it), Insights doesn't count it, Today says "No outfit recorded: this day was dressed from the sample wardrobe, which you removed." (`#nooutfit`)
- today and coming days lose their sample pieces, lock and picks (`force`) and are planned again; `tests/nu2fix3.test.js`
- the sample is never backed up (re-audit 2026-11-15, N7: loaded while connected it went into the person's real wardrobe and showed on his other phones): sync.js `syncShare` leaves out of every upload (small sync and whole backup) the sample pieces he hasn't made his (`kitEdited`); this phone's copy and the merge are unchanged
- a day record naming one goes up with the sample's pieces taken out (fix round 3, W1-3-02/W1-3-03: held back whole, a "Didn't" or a swap never reached the backup and a phone joining was told he hadn't opened the app): `syncShare` sends `kitSampleDay(rec, off, tomorrow)` (today counted as past, so his pieces stay, the sample's slots null and in `rec.gone`; a coming day loses the sample's lock and picks) with the record's own stamp, only when that still holds something of his (`kitDayOwn`: his own pieces, a pick, a lock, an occasion, a kind, a skip, a piece off, a trip)
- a day made only from the sample (`kitDayOwn` false) and one stamped `KIT_YIELD` never go up: they would replace the account's own record of that day; a whole-backup save (PUT replaces the backup) sends the backup's own copy of a day it leaves out (`syncShare(st, remote.data)`)
- except a "Didn't" / "Wore" or a correction (`worn` set, `edited`) made on such a day after this phone first synced with the account (`syncJoinAt`: localStorage `sync_join` {k: the code's last 6 characters, at}, set by `syncOnce` before its first request, `syncJoinMark`; at 1 when the phone already synced with this server, `syncMark`: in the account all along): it goes up with no outfit (`noOutfit`, `worn` kept), so a phone updated from 15 Nov with the sample in its backup gets its marks to the other phones (W1-3-02), while a phone that tried the sample before joining sends none of its sample days (W1-6-04)
- a day of sample pieces alone recorded after joining, with no mark of his, goes up with no outfit and the stamp `KIT_YIELD` (W1-3-03: a phone joining later recorded it from its own plan and said "you didn't open the app"): the backup takes it only where it has no record of the day or only the app's lock built on nothing (`dayWins`, base 0), never over a real record; the record's own stamp decides whether it is sent (`syncOrig`, `syncSentStamp` in `syncLite`'s filter)
- today and coming days (and those yielding copies) go up once per change of what he decided on them (`syncSdSig`: worn, edited, noOutfit, picks, occasion, kind, skip, off, trip, vest), not for the app's own locks and recordings: a phone with the sample and one without re-locked each other's copy at every sync (lab: a write every other background); `localStorage.sync_sd` {date: sig} is written after a save went through (`syncSdDone`), kept `SYNC_SD_DAYS` (60)
- Remove the sample gives a day it leaves with nothing of his the stamp `KIT_YIELD` (1; `kitDelMany` keeps it) instead of a new one, so the account's record of that day made on another phone wins the next merge (fix round 3, W1-6-04, Critical: a phone that tried the sample before joining stamped its sample days new and wiped the other phone's real days, past, today and locked); connected, it syncs (up to `KIT_RM_SYNC` 8 s) before Today is drawn again, so today isn't recorded from the plan over the account's record; a day with his pieces takes a new stamp as before
- a sample piece is his (`kitEdited`) only once he edits it in the item sheet: app.js `put` calls `kitUserEdit`, which sets `item.sampleMine` while the item sheet is open on that item (`editing`); a new stamp alone (a roll back, a start-up fix-up such as `migrateVests`, `checkArrivals`, `texSwatches`) no longer makes it his (re-audit 2026-11-15, Fixer 4's review: it was any stamp 3 s after `added`, so such a piece was uploaded and kept on Remove the sample)
- taking a sample piece out of rotation (or back) doesn't make it his: itemsheet.js `togglePause` sets `kitNoMine` around its `put` (re-audit 2026-11-15: paused sample shoes stayed after Remove the sample)
- the sample pieces the earlier rule counted as his stay his (fix round 3, W1-3-01: after the update they were "Sample" again and Remove the sample deleted them with their worn days): `kitSampleMigrate` (start.js, `whenPulled`, once per phone: `localStorage.kit_mine`) makes a sample piece added before `KIT_MINE_CUT` (2026-11-15 12:00 UTC, when the rule changed) and stamped more than `KIT_EDIT_MS` after `added`/`sampleBase` (`kitOldMine`, the old rule) his as Keep it as mine does (no `sample`, a new stamp, so it goes up), with a toast
- the sample banner shows only while a sample piece he hasn't made his is there (`kitHasSample`; a phone joining got "You're trying a sample wardrobe" for his own pieces)
- Remove the sample's toast has Undo (`kitSampleUndo`, `kitRmUndo`, 8 s): the pieces come back as they were with a new stamp (newer than their deletion marks), and each day still as Remove left it (`wrote`) goes back as it was, with its old stamp when it had `KIT_YIELD`; a day the account's record replaced meanwhile keeps that; the released locks stay released; `tests/samplesync.test.js`
- `kitSampleLoad` first takes in items another tab of the app stored (`kitAdopt`, from the database): a stale second tab loaded a second sample (48 pieces, on the server too)
- the setup's "Try a sample wardrobe" (setup.js `setupSample`) finishes the setup only once the sample is stored (`kitSampleDone`); finished first, a kill in between left the setup done with no sample
- import: a row matching only sample pieces is not "already in your wardrobe" (`kitDupMark` skips sample pieces he hasn't made his; re-audit 2026-11-15, N5: his rows were left unticked and gone after Remove the sample)
- import words (re-audit 2026-11-15): own colours and patterns by name through `CUSTOM_COL`/`CUSTOM_PAT` (`Object.entries` on the colours list took its index: "0 isn't one of the tie colours"); a watch's or cufflinks' colour that is a metal sets the metal (`kitMetal`: gold, rose gold → gold, silver/steel, gunmetal → 'other'), and a Metal / Buckle column is read for watches, belts and cufflinks
- import: several colours in one cell ("light blue, white", "Blue/white", "navy & burgundy & white") fill empty second and third colour columns; `kitType` finds a known word among others ("Shoes - Oxford", "Linen trouser"; last word first), plus Trouser, Oxford shirt, Suit jacket (a suit); Mauve and Fuchsia/Magenta are read
- the kit sheet's ← → step only cards: not in the import sheet (it has none: → closed it and lost the pasted rows) nor while typing in a field
- the import is a bottom sheet (2026-11-15, add-page-mockups): `kitImportOpen` sets `#kitsheet.kbs` (`kitBs`: top `--ivtop`, from 600px `max(4vh, var(--ivtop))`, at most 640px wide, rounded top, `kbsUp` slide-in), `#kit-backdrop` (a tap closes it as ✕), the grabber `.kit-grab` and the table icon (`KIT_TABLE`, `.kit-hic`) in its header, a swipe down closes it (`kitImportSwipe` → add.js `sheetSwipe`, not while writing); closing slides it away (`kitBsAway`, `.going`, emptied 320 ms later, inert meanwhile); the tour and guides stay full screen (`kitOpen` → `kitBs(false)`); its content is unchanged
- "Take them out" (`kitLastImp`) leaves imported pieces changed since the import (stamped over `KIT_EDIT_MS` after it) and says so, and is offered for `KIT_IMP_KEEP_MS` (7 days), then forgotten

- "Take them out" (`kitUnimport`) syncs first when connected (fix round 3, W1-6-05), so a piece renamed on another phone counts as changed since and stays; offline it judges this phone's copy
### `remind.js`

- morning reminder (after account.js): `#remind-box` (`.conn-only`), `remindOn`/`remindOff`, `remindTime`; per phone in `localStorage.remind`
- switching on asks permission, subscribes and posts the time, time zone and coming workdays
- `remindLines` (next 14 workdays, `piecesLine`) are kept by `remindKeep` in `dw-keep-remind` as `remind.json` for sw.js
- `remindSoon` rewrites them and tells the server only when dates or time change, or once a day (`remind_sent`)
- on iPhone Safari it asks to add to the Home Screen first (Web Push only there, iOS 16.4+)
- sw.js: `push` shows "Today’s outfit" with today's line, `notificationclick` opens the app; keep the title UTF-8 (it was once saved garbled)

### `about.js`

- Settings → About (`SET_CATS` ends with `about`, `#sc-about`, `renderAbout`): version and updates, feedback, deleting one's data, the privacy note; loaded last
- `appVersion()`: the `CACHE` number in the cached `sw.js` (`swVersionOf`, `aboutReadVersion`), so the version running, not the newest
- `checkForUpdate` (start-up, foreground after `ABOUT_FRONT_MS`, every `ABOUT_CHECK_MS`, the button): `aboutLatest` fetches `sw.js` with `cache:'no-store'`; a higher number downloads it and offers Update once per version (`aboutToasted`, `aboutShow`); never reloads by itself
- `updateNow()`: asks first only if a photo on Add is still being read (below), sends the waiting worker `{type:'skipWaiting'}` and reloads on `controllerchange`; gives up after `ABOUT_INSTALL_MS` (45 s) without reloading
- `aboutAdopt()`: a waiting worker of the version already running takes over without a reload (`aboutAsk`)
- feedback (`sendFeedback`): `POST /feedback` with `x-app-token` (`aboutConn`); `loadFeedback` lists it in `#fb-list` for the owner only
- delete my data: `askDelete` (`#del-confirm`), `deleteMyData(server)` → `wipeDevice()` → reload
- `wipeDevice()`: deletes the IndexedDB database (`ABOUT_DB`), the `dw-keep-*` caches, online also the service worker and its caches, and the app's own localStorage keys (`ABOUT_KEYS`, `ABOUT_KEY_PREFIXES`); never `localStorage.clear()`, as GitHub Pages sites of one account share an origin
- a new localStorage key must be added to `ABOUT_KEYS`: `tests/about.test.js` fails otherwise
- "Delete everything" sends `DELETE /account` first with the connection removed and sync stopped; a 404 clears the device and says the server copy remains; any other failure restores the connection and deletes nothing
- deleting on the device only also removes the connection, or the next sync would bring the wardrobe back
- the after-restart message goes in `sessionStorage.about_note` (`ABOUT_NOTE`), shown by `aboutStart()`
- the privacy note (`.about-fold`) names everyone who gets something: the server's owner, its hosting company (unnamed) and GitHub Pages, Anthropic, OpenAI, Open-Meteo, Nager.Date / Google
- `tests/about.test.js` mocks `/me`, `/feedback`, `/account`
- `updateNow` asks only while a photo on Add is still being read (`busy`/`multiBusy`); other cards come back after the restart (add.js `addKeep`, called before it), so "they will be lost" is gone (re-audit 2026-11-15)
- Feedback: one send at a time (`fbSending`), and an empty tap within `FB_AGAIN_MS` (3 s) of a send keeps "Thank you" (a double tap read "Write something first."); the day's saves used up (429 `kv-limit`) says to send it again after the reset (`aboutResetAt`), not the backup sentence "kept on this device"
- an ended invitation's Delete card lists this device only, says the server is out of reach and offers only "Delete from this device"; its note doesn't say the server's backup was kept
- a 403 'forbidden token' to a resumed "Delete everything" (`aboutDeleteAccount(keep, true)`), or for a code remembered as a tester's (`aboutKeptTester`: `acct_role.tok` = the kept code), is the deletion having gone through: at start-up the connection is already removed, so `isTester()` said no and a phone killed mid-delete reopened with "nothing was deleted" (re-audit 2026-11-15, agent8 #5)
- refusals are said with the server's `message`, or an older server's sentence in `error`, never a code (`aboutErrText`, `ABOUT_CODES`: "invite off", "forbidden token", "kv-limit" reached testers); Feedback after an ended invitation says so and Send is off (`aboutLive`: `isConnected()`)
- a tester's "Delete everything" says it ends the invitation (the server deletes `invite:<hash>` first, so the link stops working on every phone)
- "Delete everything" keeps `del_pending` ({w, t}) and clears the connection's database copy (`connKeep`) before `DELETE /account` (`aboutDeleteAccount`); a phone killed before the answer finishes it at the next start (`aboutResumeDelete`, from `aboutStart`; app.js `connRestored` restores nothing while it is set), or, the server unreachable, puts the connection back and says so; killed there it used to reopen with the whole wardrobe and "Connected"
- a 403 'forbidden token' to "Delete everything" (the account already deleted on another phone) clears the device and says the server's data was already deleted
- `ABOUT_KEYS` has `tour_seen`, `tex_up`, `photo_up`, `asm_gl` (kept through constants, which `tests/about.test.js` didn't see; `tests/invitefix.test.js` resolves `const X='key'`) and `del_pending`
- "Delete everything" refused for the day's saves (429 `kv-limit`) says "The server can’t delete anything more today, so nothing was deleted. Try again after <local reset time>…" (`aboutDeleteAccount`, W1-10-01)
- the privacy note doesn't name the server's host (W1-10-06, the round's rule: nothing a tester reads says Cloudflare): "It runs with a cloud hosting company"; `tests/about.test.js`

### `stats.js`

- the stats page (the Insights tab, `#n-stats`, `openStats`/`renderStats`, shown as the `#stats` section by `show('stats')`): `buildStats()` counts what was really worn (`wornDays()`: day records from when the wardrobe existed, not days marked "Not worn") and what is planned for the next six months (`plannedDays()`)
- sections: 'Your scores', wear counts by type (folded sections build their rows when opened), what costs points, colour balance, pairings and rest, never-worn pieces with a reason, photo tagging (no learning section: removed 2026-11-15)
- 'Your scores' (`#st-trend`): the average score of each of the last `TREND_WEEKS` (8) worn weeks as columns (red when a day had a hard check), with the worn average, the days with a real mistake and the plan's average
- the next six months are planned a week at a time while the phone is idle (`statsPlan`, `STATS_SLICE` 7 days of the shared simulation per `requestIdleCallback`), started 4 s after start-up and by opening the tab (opening Insights used to freeze a phone 1.3 s)
- `show('stats')` calls `statsShow`, which draws the page at once when the months are planned (`statsPlanned`; always with `TEST_PLAN`), else the header and "Planning your next 6 months…" (`.st-wait`) and the rest when done; `renderStats()` called directly still plans at once
- pieces the dress code leaves out are not counted as never worn (`buildStats().unused`)
- pieces are called by the name the user gave, exactly as typed (`statName`, `statName1`, `statName2`), else their description; the tiles say "1 item", "1 outfit", "different outfits planned" (exact pieces; What to buy counts "colour combinations")
- 'Your scores' leaves out the weeks that ended before the wardrobe existed (`planStartDay`), so a new wardrobe shows one or two columns, not six empty ones
- an empty wardrobe's Insights, Packing list and Laundry & cleaning pages offer "Add your first item" and "Try a sample wardrobe" (`emptyAdd`)
- `whyUnused` works out each jacket's best outfit (`allOf`: its `FIT_PANTS` (3) best trousers x its `FIT_SHIRTS` (8) best shirts x every tie) and its best with the one shirt or tie asked about (`withOf`), only for pieces that need a reason, kept in `fitMemo` until the plan cache (`cache`) is replaced, the item count or the settings it reads change (`fitMemoFor`, `fitSettingsSig`); every jacket x trousers x shirt x tie, rebuilt on every `renderStats`, froze Insights 4.5 s at 300 items on opening and on every fold (W2-18-02, fix round 3)
- unfolding a Wear counts section fills only its rows (`statsToggle` → `statRowsHtml` from `statsLast`, the numbers the page was drawn from, while `cache` and the item count are unchanged), not the whole page; `tests/fix8r3.test.js`
- Colour balance lists an owner's colour as its own row by its own name ("Dusty pink"), not the group of the colour it behaves like (W2-17-04)
- 'Your scores' names each week by its first workday on or after the day the wardrobe began (`t.label`): "Nov 13", a Friday before the first day, read as scores from before the app was installed (W2-15-08)
- `whyUnused`: a paused item was taken out of rotation; an item on the way says so, with its arrival date if there is one; a piece away says "It's at the cleaner's" / "It's in the laundry", with the date it joins the outfits again; a dress-code exclusion says so ("Your dress code leaves out ties…")
- `whyUnused` never says a suiting tie, shirt or pair of trousers "comes up later than six months" (it can't know that); it says the others suit a little better or have rested longer, so it isn't in the next six months' plan
- Wear counts: a type the dress code leaves out (`dressOut`: blazers and suits with Jackets off) says "left out by your dress code", never "N never worn" (re-audit 2026-11-15); `tests/nu2fix7.test.js`
- `plannedDays()` takes What to buy's days (`statsDays` = analysis.js `horizonKeys(today, SHOP_WEEKS)`: the workdays of 26 weeks from tomorrow), so Insights' "outfits planned" and What to buy's "workdays" are the same number (they were 122 and 126); `statsMonths` reaches the horizon's last day
- a wardrobe that can't dress a day yet (`statsShort`: setup.js `setupNeeds` rows not ticked, as Today's checklist) gets no plan numbers: the tiles keep items and days worn, a "Not a full outfit yet" card (`#st-short`) names what is missing, and Never worn, Small deductions, Colour balance, Pairings and the plan's average are left out (it said "121 outfits planned … 50.0 average" for a suit and a shirt)

### `pack.js`

- the packing list page (Settings → Packing list `#pack-btn`; the month on Today has no link to it, the owner took that button out): the `#pack` section, shown by `show('pack')` through `packShow(t)` (called at the end of `show()`), `openPack`/`renderPack`
- the trip (`packTrip()`, `localStorage.pack_trip` `{a, b, j, s}`: first and last day as `YYYY-MM-DD`, most jackets and pairs of shoes, 0 = no limit) defaults to the workdays of the week starting on the user's next first workday (`packDefault`, `packWeekStart`: a workday whose day before is not one; Sunday for Sunday-Thursday, Monday for Monday-Friday or when there is no such day)
- a stored trip that is over makes way for the default; at most `PACK_MAX` (31) days (`packSetDate`)
- `packDays(t, plan)`: every day of the range with its outfit from `planMonth` (or from a plan passed in), `o` null on a day off or a day with nothing planned; the page lists each with the month's stack drawing (`stackSVG`) and `shortTop`/`shortRest`
- `packList(days, wear)` → `{groups, worn, counts, total}`: every distinct piece once with its days (`n`), grouped by type in `TYPES` order (`PACK_TITLE`); a suit's `o.bot` is the suit itself and is not counted again; a suit worn with its vest says so
- one belt per shoe colour worn (`leatherGroup` of the shoes): the belt planned most often with that colour, so two brown belts planned on different days are one line
- what the wardrobe lacks is a line without an item (keys `x-belt-<group>`, `x-links`, `x-coat`): a belt for shoes with none that matches, cufflinks for a French-cuff shirt, a coat on a cold or wet day
- the count line (`packCount`, `PACK_COUNTED`: suits, blazers, dress pants, shirts, ties, shoes, socks; nouns in `PACK_NOUN`)
- ticks and travel wear are kept per trip on the device (`localStorage.pack_marks`: `{'<a>_<b>': {t:[line keys], w:[item ids], at}}`, the newest `PACK_TRIPS` 8; `packMark`/`packSetMark`, `packTick`, `packWear`)
- a piece marked Wear moves to "Wearing on the way" and is not counted; `packWearDay` marks the first outfit day's whole outfit (`packWornIds`: its belt is the list's belt for that shoe colour)
- pack lighter (`packLight(t, base)`, memoised on the `cache` object): with a limit below what the trip uses, the trip's days are planned again on a copy of the day records (`packPlanOf(recs, end)`: `planSim` from the shared start with `dayRecs` swapped, like `planWith` in today.js)
- `packLightRecs(days, AJ, AS)`: each live day rules out (`rec.skip.top` / `.shoes`) every jacket of its kind and every pair of shoes not in the allowed sets, drops its lock, and takes the other kind of jacket (`rec.kind`) when none of its own kind is allowed and the day has no occasion
- pieces picked by hand (`rec.force`) and days already worn are never changed and come first in the allowed sets (`packTally`)
- up to `PACK_TRIES` (3) sets of jackets × 3 sets of shoes are tried, the most worn first (`packSets`), with one suit and one blazer first when the limit is 2+ and the trip has both; measured 100 ms on the test wardrobe
- the best set by `packRate` wins: outfit scores, −50 for a hard check, −1000 for an incomplete outfit, −3000 for each piece over the limit
- the page shows the lighter plan's outfits and list with what it saves (`packSaved`) and the average score before and after; nothing is saved until "Keep this plan" (`packLock` → `packLockDays`, which also saves `rec.kind` for a day that took the other kind)
- `packLockDays` gives every live day of the trip `rec.lock = lockIds(o)` (the app's own lock only covers 7 days); `packLockState` counts the days whose lock is what the plan shows; the page says Locked / n of m days locked / Not locked; `packRelease` removes the locks
- a kept trip's days carry `rec.trip` (`packLockDays`): `clearLocks()` (a planner setting, an occasion) and new planning rules (`replanFuture(true)`) leave them locked, so the packed suitcase stays right; "Start the plan over" (`replanFuture()`) and "Let the plan change again" (`packRelease`) release them (the lock was silently dropped by any Settings change, audit 2026-11-14)
- a piece that won't be here by the trip's first day (on the way or away being cleaned, `!inRotation(i, first day)`: `packLate`) is left out of the trip's plan (`packLateRecs`: `rec.skip` on a copy of the records, used by Pack lighter too) and the Outfits card says so (`#pk-late`)
- `packText(t, days, list)` is the plain-text list (title, count line, groups, travel wear, the outfits per day); `packCopy` uses `navigator.clipboard`, falling back to a hidden textarea; Share (`packShare`) only where `navigator.share` exists
- `tests/pack.test.js`; `PACK_SHOTS=<folder>` saves pictures of the page at 320/390/626px in both themes

### `wardrobe.js`

- search still finds the British words: "grey" matches the gray pieces and "trousers" the pants (`wardHay` appends them)
- search finds a material by its other words (`matSyn`, from core.js `MAT_ALIAS`; 2026-11-15): "fresco" or "high-twist" finds tropical wool, "lisle" mercerized cotton, "broadcloth" poplin, "nylon" a performance stretch shirt; a stored name off the list also finds by the listed name it means ("merino wool" finds socks typed "Merino"); a narrower word ("knitted silk" for silk) is left out
- the one-time rename (`matMigrate`, `MAT_RENAME`, 2026-11-15, the owner's decision on the materials audit): a shirt whose material is exactly "nylon/spandex" or "nylon" (any case: his 10 stretch shirts) becomes "performance stretch", read from the stored wardrobe, saved with a new stamp (`putRaw`, not `put`: a sample piece doesn't become his) and synced (`scheduleSync`); run after the first pull (`whenPulled`) and when a sync brings such a shirt in (dress.js `dressSynced`); idempotent; in the app rather than written to the server because it is his own wording (the wardrobe-edit rule: through the normal sync path)
- the Wardrobe tab's search and sort, the item fields `name` / `brand` / `material` / `notes`, and Delete with Undo (loaded after stats.js; called from `renderWardrobe`, `wardList`, `itemBody` and `rm` in itemsheet.js and `save` in add.js)
- a grid box's accessible name ends with its tag (`giTag`/`giNote`: Sample, Out of rotation, On the way, at the cleaner's, due for cleaning): the `aria-label` hid the visible tag (re-audit 2026-11-15)
- search: a field above the type chips (`wardBar`, `#wsearch`, `wardQ`); every word typed must be in the item's name, brand, notes, material, type, colour names or pattern (`wardHay`, `wardMatch`; case and accents ignored); it combines with the type chip and stays when the chip changes
- typing redraws only the grid and the count (`wardSearch` → `wardRefresh`: `#wslide`, `#wcount`, `#wclear`), never the field, so the focus and the keyboard stay; a full `renderWardrobe` while the field is in use (a sync arriving) puts the focus and the caret back (`wardKeep`/`wardBack`)
- the (screen-reader) count reads "n items", or "n of m items" when a chip or the search narrows the list (`wardCount`); × in the field clears it (`wardClear`, also Escape)
- nothing matches: `#wnone` (`wardGrid`) says so, with "Clear search" and, when a chip is on and other types match, how many and "Search all types"
- sort: the dropdown beside the search field (`#wsort`, `WARD_SORTS`, `setWardSort`), remembered in `localStorage.ward_sort`: By type, Newest first, Most worn, Longest since worn (never worn first), Colour, A to Z (by the title shown)
- By type follows the type chips' order (`Object.keys(TYPES)`, suits first), newest first within a type
- Most worn and Longest since worn count the days really worn (`wardWear` over `wornDays()` in stats.js) and each box says "Worn n times" / "Last worn 3 Nov" / "Not worn yet" (`.wwear`)
- Colour groups by family (`WARD_FAMS`: neutrals, blues, browns, greens, reds, purples, then pieces shown by their metal; `WARD_FAM` over the planner's `fam`), light to dark within each (`lumCol`, the item's `shade` counted)
- `wardView()` is the list on screen (chip, search, sort) and `wardList()` (itemsheet.js) returns it, so the item sheet's ‹ ›, swipes and "n of m" follow the filtered, sorted list; it is kept in `wardMemo` until the next redraw (`wardFresh`), since the sheet's swipe asks for it on every touch move
- `wardGrid(list)` draws the grid: a box's title is `itemTitle(i)`
- fields: `item.name` (60 characters), `item.brand` (40), `item.material` (40), `item.notes` (500) (`WARD_FIELDS`, `WARD_MAX`); an empty one is removed from the item
- the item sheet's Details card has Name and Brand at its top (`wardFieldsTop`), Material and Notes at its end (`wardFieldsEnd`)
- `setItemField` saves a text field when it is left and 600 ms after the last keystroke (`itemFieldSoon`: a sheet swiped away never tells a field it was left) without redrawing the sheet (the next field tapped would lose its focus); only the header's title and the grid follow
- Material (`matRow`) is None, the type's `MATERIALS_BY` list, Other; Other shows "Which material?" and the text typed is the material (`'other'` until something is typed); types without a list show no Material row
- on an Add card, a material photo tagging filled in (tagging.js `takeMaterial`, `q.matAI`) is said under the field ("Material suggested by Claude (medium confidence): …. Change it if it’s wrong.", `.matai`) while the field still holds it; choosing one by hand (`setQField`) drops the mark and sets `q.matSet`, so a later reading leaves it
- the Add card has "More details" (`moreRow`, `toggleMore`, `q.more`), folded until tapped, with the same four fields (`setQField`, kept on the card as typed); folded with something in it, it says "n filled in"; `wardSaveFields(q,it)` in `save()` copies them onto the item
- a name is the item's title in the Wardrobe grid, the item sheet's header, Today's pieces list and the piece picker (and their `aria-label`s)
- the fields are ordinary item properties, so they sync, back up, restore and roll back with the item (items merge field by field: core.js mergeItem)
- Delete (`rm` → `wardDelete`) doesn't ask: it removes the item at once, closes the sheet and shows the toast "Deleted <name>" with Undo for `WARD_UNDO_MS` (8 s)
- Undo (`wardUndelete`) writes the item back as it was (same id, photos, close-up, tags, fields, rotation state, its place in `items`) with `updated` one above the deletion's time, and saves again any day record that referred to it and was changed meanwhile (a day re-planned without it)
- why that survives a sync: the tombstone stays, and in `mergeState` a tombstone only wins over an item whose stamp is not newer; so the restored item wins on every device, also on one that had already taken the deletion; a delete that is not undone propagates as before
- `tests/wardrobe.test.js`: search by each field and with a chip, each sort order, the sheet stepping through the filtered list, the fields (sheet, Add card, two synced devices, backup file restored on an empty device), the name in its four places, materials by type, delete / Undo with and without a sync in between, sizes and no sideways scroll at 320 / 390 / 626px in both themes
- Material (`matRow`, types in `MATERIALS_BY`) sits on the Add card above "More details" and at the end of the item sheet's Details
- a text field's typing is also kept in `localStorage.ward_pending` `{id,k,v,was,at}` at each keystroke and saved at once on `pagehide` / the page hidden (`wardFlush`; 2026-11-15: a name typed then Safari swiped away within 0.6 s was lost on a kill, a hidden page's timers stop); `wardPendingRestore` (from `safetyStart`, after the first pull) puts a copy left by a kill back when that field still holds `was` (another device's change since wins), and drops it otherwise
- Delete and Duplicate ignore a second tap while the first runs (`wardOnce`, `wardBusy`, 400 ms after; `wardDelete` → `wardDeleteNow`, `wardDuplicate` → kit.js `kitDuplicate`, one key for Duplicate since the sheet moves to the copy)

### `colpick.js`

- the colour picker (loaded after wardrobe.js): the Colour, Second colour / Toe cap and Third colour fields of the Add card and the item sheet are a button with a chip and the name (`colBtn`, `EC` in the editor), not a `<select>`: the owner couldn't tell what the colour names looked like
- a tap opens `#col-sheet` (z-index 1201, above the item sheet and the pickers): the item's close-up (or photo; a Close-up / Whole photo switch when it has both) edge to edge with a large chip of the colour being looked at, kept at the top while the chips scroll
- under it: "Closest to your close-up/photo" (main colour only, from `item.meas` via `nearestCols`: Very close ≤4 ΔE, Close ≤8, Nearest), then every colour `COLOR_OPTS` allows for the type, grouped by family from Lab (`colFamily`: chroma under 9 neutral, then hue bands) and light to dark; accents start with None
- "Closest to your close-up" uses the measured parts (`item.meas.parts`) for a patterned cloth, and offers nothing for one without them
- an item without a photo shows its drawing in the sheet, redrawn in the colour being looked at (`renderColPick`, the owner's ask); a New chip (Claude's own colour) keeps the item's colour there
- a tap on a chip only previews it; the bottom button "Use <colour>" / "Keep <colour>" saves through `setq` (Add card) or `sete` (editor); Escape closes it without leaving the item (window keydown in the capture phase)
- the pattern field is the same kind of button (`patBtn`, `EP` in the editor; a small drawn swatch, `swatchSVG(patVar(…),true)`) and opens the same sheet with k 'pattern' (`renderPatPick`): the close-up beside a large drawing of the item in the pattern looked at (`itemThumb(patVar(it,p),true)`: another pattern is drawn without the item's reading), then every pattern `PATS_BY` allows, drawn, grouped (`PAT_GROUPS`: plain, stripes, checks, weaves, figures, other)
- without a photo the pattern sheet's left side is the item's shaped drawing (`itemThumb(patVar(it,drawAs(sel)))`: a tie, a jacket's panel) in the pattern being looked at, beside the large swatch (the owner's ask)
- Ask Claude (`colAskRun`, `colAskHtml`): with a close-up, the sheet offers to have Claude read the colours again (cost from `benchEstimate(1)`); the request is the tagger's (`ask`) with the white-balanced close-up, the sharp photo (`tagImgOf`, else the photo) and the close-up's measurement, like `refineFromCloseup`
- without a close-up it says to add one first; not connected, the button is off with `NEED_CONN`
- the answer is kept in memory per item (`colAsk`, not saved or synced), so every colour field of that item shows it without asking again; Claude's pick for the field is selected beside the close-up, its runner-up (`altColor`) shown next to it with the confidence and any `newColor`; nothing is saved until "Use …" or "Use all …"
- any colour: the picker's ask sends `freeColors:true` (`tagExtras`); the worker then adds `ownColors {primary, secondary, tertiary: {name, hex} | null}` to the schema and prompt (`FREE_EXTRA`, `OWN_COLORS`, tidied in `cleanTag`; worker/tag.js), Claude's own names and shades not limited to the list; the app keeps them as `ownColors` (`ownColorsOf` in `normTag`, app.js); a normal tag doesn't ask for them
- Ask Claude from the pattern sheet sends `freePattern:true`: the worker adds `ownPattern {name, description, fits}` (`OWN_PATTERN`, `FREE_PAT_EXTRA`, tidied in `cleanTag`; worker/tag.js), Claude's own precise name for the pattern and whether the listed one names it fully
- one own reading per request: a colour sheet sends `freeColors`, the pattern sheet `freePattern`, never both (answers kept apart, `colAsk['<id>|c']` / `colAsk['<id>|p']`); with both the schema is more than the API compiles ("compiled grammar is too large", found on the live worker 2026-11-13), and worker.js drops `freeColors` when both come (a phone with the older app)
- a pattern Claude named that the list lacks (`patNewFor`: the listed one doesn't name it fully and no pattern of that name exists) is a "New · Claude's name" chip drawn like Claude's listed pick, beside "Closest you have" and Claude's second choice
- "Add <name> to your patterns and use it" (`patAddNew`): `addCustomPattern` (synced) with Claude's description, drawn and planned like Claude's listed pick (`patNewBase`), for suits, blazers and trousers together or the item's own type; then set like any pattern; `tests/colpick.test.js`, `tests/claude.test.js`
- a colour Claude named that the app lacks (`colNewFor`: not a built-in's name, not the owner's own key, not within ΔE00 2 of the listed pick) is offered first as a "New · Claude's exact" chip in Claude's shade (`NEW_COL` '+new'), with "Closest you have" beside it; the listed pick stays selected, so a colour is only added when the owner picks it
- "Add <name> to your colours and use it" (`colAddNew`): `addCustomColor` (synced, `settings.custom`) for the item's type group (`groupOf`), behaving like the nearest built-in colour (`colBaseFor`, as `openColForm`), an accent colour too when it was a second or third colour; then set like any colour
- "Use both colours" / "Use all N colours" (`colAskUseAll`, `.cpall`): shown whenever Claude named more than one colour and any field, the open one included, would change (`colAllDiff`); it takes every colour Claude named, its new ones too (`colAllOf`: each added through `colAddNew`; one already added is the owner's colour of that name), on the Add card and in the editor; the owner wanted to accept all of Claude's colours at once
- `tests/colpick.test.js`
- which colour fields show (`colShows`, `colRows`, `colFieldLabel`; 2026-11-15, the new-user audit): the main colour always but on a watch on a metal bracelet (on a leather strap "Strap colour"); a second colour for patterned cloth, socks ("Toe cap") and sneakers ("Sole"); a third only for patterned cloth; in the item sheet a field already holding a colour stays so it can be cleared; the Shade row hides with the main colour
- `colTidy(it)` drops a second or third colour the type can't show, at Confirm (`saveCard`) and on a type change (`typeTidy`, itemsheet.js)
- "+ A colour of my own" (`colOwnHtml`, `colOwnOpen`, `colOwnSave`) was replaced by the "+ New colour" sheet (2026-11-15)
- the New colour sheet's drawn item follows the finger on the square and the hue bar and a valid hex as typed (`cnFigLive`, called from `cnLive`; the owner's ask, 2026-11-15): only `.cpph.cpfig .cpdraw` is redrawn (`cnDrawn`), at most once a frame (requestAnimationFrame, skipped when the shade is unchanged), about 3-5 ms at 4× CPU throttling and 1-2 ms in WebKit; the whole sheet is still drawn on lift; `tests/colpicklive.test.js`
- "+ New colour" / "+ New pattern or texture" (2026-11-15, the owner's approved design in `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\`): states of `#col-sheet` (`colPick.newc` = {kind, from 'pick'|'add'|'settings', hex, h/s/v, name, desc, state, res, base, types, accent, look, closeOn}), `openColNew`/`renderColNew`, `openPatNew`/`renderPatNew`, ‹ back to the list (`colNewBack`; Escape goes back first, then closes)
- the pickers start with "Your colours" / "Your patterns" (`.cnown`): the owner's own for this item, then the dashed "+ New" tile (`#cn-open`, `#pn-open`); own colours and patterns also stay in their family / pattern group (the owner's choice: both places)
- "Describe it to Claude" (`describeAsk` → worker `POST /describe`): the words (≤120, no `<>`), the item's kind and colours, `settings.custom`, and on the pattern sheet the close-up when "Also show Claude my close-up" is ticked (`#pn-close`); busy, result, error (`#cn-err`), another kind (`#cn-kind`: "That sounds like a pattern" → Open patterns, `cnSwitch`) and limit (`.cnlim`) states
- the reuse thresholds (the owner's choice): ΔE00 ≤ 3 "That's your X", "Use X" first (`state 'dup'`); 3-6 Claude's choice first, the other second; over 6 a new colour is the default; a pattern Claude says is already listed ("existing" and in the item's choices) is `state 'reuse'`, "Use X" first
- "Pick the shade": a square (saturation across, brightness down) and a hue bar with pointer capture, arrow keys by 1, and a hex field, kept in step (`cnSetHsv`, `cnLive` while a finger moves, a redraw when it lifts; `cnKeyUp` keeps the slider's focus)
- the cost line: the owner "About N¢" (`describeEstimate`, tagging.js), a tester "N of M lookups left today" from `/me` (`cnLeft`; no cents for testers); none left or not connected: Ask is off
- "Add … and use it" (`colNewAdd`): `addCustomColor({name, hex, base, types, accent})` / `addCustomPattern({…, look})`, then `cnDone` sets the field as `useColPick` does (on the Add card it clears the card's `newColor`/`newPattern`); "Use X instead" is `cnUseExisting`
